# KnifeCraft — Handoff (Playgama edition)

- Cleaning is its own round BUTTON in the Kitchen's right-hand column, right under Mini-games (developer 2026-11-22, third time of asking, with a screenshot of the wide Kitchen: "I need it under mini games button as a separate section from mini games and business"; the card and then the tab inside the sheet were NOT what was meant). `Kitchen.tsx`: after Mini-games a fifth pill `cleaning-shortcut` with the painted broom (`PaintedPic name="broom"`), the same tap-to-expand / tap-again-to-open as Inventory and Business, a red "!" (`cleaning-shortcut-badge`) while jobs wait (`cleanlinessOf(save).tasks`), a lock and "after the next service" below `CLEANING_FROM_LEVEL` (4; a second tap goes nowhere). It opens `business-cleanliness` (own header, no Business tab strip, Back to the Kitchen). Room for five buttons on a short upright phone: from 700 px of height the column uses 48 px pills (was 56), under 620 px it starts at 70 px with a 2 px gap and the day strip is limited to `calc(100% - 72px)` so the column ends above the Next Customer card (320x568 is tight). The Mini-games sheet's Cleaning TAB (previous entry) is kept as a second way in; say if it should go. Test: `tools/e2e/cleaningrail.mjs` (order and badge, expand then open, Back, locked below Level 4, in view with nothing on top of it at 1815x867, 1280x720, 768x1024, 430x900, 390x844, 360x640, 320x568 and 740x360). RULE CHANGE in `portraitonly.mjs` P3: the sink is now reached through the Cleaning button (the Business > Cleanliness tab it used had left in the previous commit). `minigamesaudit` has one flaky screenshot (Chrome "Unable to capture screenshot") and needs the root `out/` folder to exist.

- CHEF PRIDE, Phase 7: measuring (developer 2026-11-23 "start building the next phase"), UNCOMMITTED. Added the events the plan promised and did not have yet: `record_broken {kind: perfects|combo|speed}` for a plate (Preparation; the service one was already in App), `dare_offered {id}` (the Kitchen, once per day and app run), `dare_declined {id}` (`dare_taken`, `dare_completed`, `title_earned`, `rival_passed` existed; Paige's rival message is the existing `assistant_beat_shown {beat: rival}`). New `docs/planning/CHEF-PRIDE-MEASUREMENT-2026-11-23.md` (the event table, the baseline, how to read it with the Playgama telemetry tools, the revised decision rule) and `scripts/chef-pride-analytics-qa.mts` (the code and the document list the same events, snake_case, flat data, no personal / free-text / money field, one way out through `sendAnalyticsEvent`, sent from the right places). WHAT THE BASELINE SAID (Playgama telemetry 2026-10-24..11-23, read 2026-11-23): 260 sessions / 230 users in 31 days, only 2-4 sessions a day since 10 Nov, median session 56 s, median level 44 s, median furthest level 1 (90th percentile 22), 7 rewarded-ad opens in a month, and EVERY event is `game_version` 1.4.1: no Chef Pride build has reached players (the repo is 1.6.0, no zip uploaded). So: nothing is measurable yet, a two-week percentage rule (day-1 / day-7) would be noise, and the ego features sit at Level 3-8 where only about the top tenth of an already tiny audience arrives. The decision rule was REWRITTEN from percentages to counts per `game_version` once a build has 200 sessions (reach of at least 1 in 10 sessions, dare acceptance and completion, nothing regresses). Phases 4-6 (prestige, local league, share cards) are therefore NOT built: they wait on that data (and the lever that probably matters more is traffic and the first minute). Tests: `chef-pride-analytics-qa` ALL PASS; `dares.mjs` and `chefrecords.mjs` pass.

- CHEF PRIDE, Phase 3b: the optional daily dare (developer 2026-11-23 "start building the next phase"), UNCOMMITTED, together with Phase 3a. `restaurant/skillChallenges.ts` (pure; the day key is a parameter): ONE dare a day, OPT-IN, picked by a fixed hash of the calendar day (`hashString` of `DailyOrderManager` is now exported) among the dares unlocked at the player's level; the same dare for everyone that day. FIVE dares with exact rules: No Mercy Prep (5 plates in a row at Clean, counted from taking it), Perfect Chef (3 Masterful in a row), Speed Demon (one Clean plate of 6+ cuts in under 30 s of unpaused time), Zero Waste (close a business day with no spoiled stock), Clean Freak (a spotless service). UNLOCKS follow the game's own systems, not the plan's guess: the three plate dares from Level 6 (`DARES_FROM_LEVEL`), Zero Waste with the fridge-and-freshness system (Level 10 today) and Clean Freak with dine-in (Level 12 today) (`restaurantSystem(..).firstLevel`; the plan said 21 / 31, the progression file says 10 / 12). Kitchen Chaos stays deferred. State: `chefRecord.challenge {day, id, state accepted|declined|won, progress}` + `chefRecord.daresWon`; a state from another day is ignored (a new day starts fresh; nothing carries, nothing is punished). Winning pays a BADGE only: a new four-tier "dares won" ladder (3/10/30/100: Dare Taker, Daredevil, Fearless Chef, Dare Legend; reward 0, ids `ach-dares-<n>`), no money, no ledger, no ad. Where it shows: the Kitchen's Next Customer DETAILS (`DareRow`, `kitchen-dare`, Take the dare / Not today, both 48 px; the next rival's chef "dares you" from Level 8, else "Today's dare") with a small marker on View Details while it is still offered; the Knife Report adds `Dare won: ...` / `Dare: 3 of 5 plates in a row` / `Dare: the run ended at 4. Try again?` for an accepted plate dare (`Preparation` prop `dareLine`); the Level Complete card adds `Dare won: X`. Wiring in `App.tsx`: `persist()` moves the dare with the plate in the same save, `completeCampaignLevel` moves Clean Freak, and BOTH day-close sites now call `closeDayDared` (the same `closeDay` plus a look at spoilage for Zero Waste); `handleDare` takes or declines; analytics `dare_taken {id}` and `dare_completed {id}`. `useToday` moved from `DailyGiftCard` to its own `kc/useToday.ts` (the Kitchen shares it). RULE CHANGES in existing suites (said in the comments): `restaurant-day-qa` W2 and `closing-chores-qa` U3 accept `closeDayDared(`, `phase5-qa` B16 reads the hook from `useToday.ts` and E4 allows the `hashString` export. Fixed on the way: Paige's spotless line keeps its precedence over the rank line on a combined celebration (bug-batch-qa 3e), `persist` keeps its original `syncKitchenUpgradeOwnership(next)` line (progression-preview-qa K11 had been failing since the Phase 0 commit), and the oven panel's testid is `oven-panel` (oven-step-qa F2 forbids the word `controls` in `OvenStep.tsx`; it had been failing since the oven commit). STALE, not mine, left for the developer: `oven-step-qa` E2 (a diff audit since commit ae4ad52) still fails because of four OTHER files changed by earlier commits (`ambientDiners.ts`, `cleaningDirt.ts`, `quickRestock.ts` which touches money, `staffLifeWide.ts`); this phase's four new pure files are added to its exclusion list with a RULE CHANGE comment. Tests: new `scripts/skill-challenges-qa.mts` (A-I) and `tools/e2e/dares.mjs` (offered with the marker, Take saves it and moves no money, Not today stays hidden after a reload, none below Level 6, a REAL plate on a taken No Mercy Prep at 4 of 5 shows the dare line and saves the win, fits 320x568 / 390x844 / 1280x720): ALL PASS. NOT seen in the browser: the Zero Waste and Clean Freak wins (a day close and a spotless service; covered by the QA), and the Level Complete "Dare won" note.

- CHEF PRIDE, Phase 3a: chef titles (developer 2026-11-23 "start building the next phase"), UNCOMMITTED. `progression/chefTitles.ts` (pure): six identities x four tiers, DRAFT names and thresholds for the developer to tune after the first release's data (all in `CHEF_IDENTITIES`): Precision (perfect cuts 25/100/300/800: Steady Hand, Precision Chef, Master of the Edge, Knife Legend), Speed (Clean plates under 30 s 10/40/120/300: Quick Hands, Line Sprinter, Speed Chef, Lightning Chef), Organizer (Fridge Sort 3-star crates 5/20/60/150), Cleanliness (spotless services 5/20/60/150), Customer Favourite (popularity 65/80/92/100), Restaurateur (places climbed in the city ranking 25/40/47/49). New counter `chefRecord.quickClean` (a Clean-or-better plate of 3+ cuts, 4 s+ and under 30 s of unpaused time). They are BADGE-ONLY achievements (`reward: 0`, ids `ach-chef-<identity>-<target>`, 24 of them, appended to `achievements.ts` as `CHEF_LADDERS`), so they list in the Achievements tab and move no money (`collectableTotal` unchanged; achievement-rewards, economy-v25, fridge-sort, idle and final-economy suites pass untouched). The title WORN is derived, never stored: the highest tier earned in any identity (a tie goes to the first identity listed); the bar on Progress follows that identity, or, before the first title, the closest one to its first tier. Shown: `ChefTitleCard` on Restaurant Progress under the city ranking (`chef-title-card`; from Level 3, `CHEF_TITLES_FROM_LEVEL`; six identity chips with stars), a title chip beside the rank on the Kitchen rank card on windows 700+ px tall (`kitchen-chef-title`), the Achievements list, and an announcement when a service ends: `announceTitles` marks every earned tier as seen (`chefRecord.seen`, `title:<identity>:<target>`) and tells the player about the best new one only (an old save that earned several is told about one): the Level Complete card gets a "Title earned: X" note, the celebration card a new kind `title` ("New title: X!", `🏅 New title: X (Identity)`, one card with a rank rise too), analytics `title_earned {identity, tier}`. OPEN QUESTION still for the developer: automatic title (built) or player-chosen among the earned ones. Tests: new `scripts/chef-titles-qa.mts` (A-H) and `tools/e2e/titles.mjs` (Progress card, none below Level 3, Kitchen chip tall/short, Achievements, fits 320/390/1280): ALL PASS. NOT seen in the browser: the title celebration card and the Level Complete note (a service end; covered by `chef-titles-qa` D/H and the wiring checks). `tools/e2e/chefrecords.mjs` is FLAKY: the cutting solver sometimes never reaches the Knife Report (one run in three failed checks 1 and 2, the next passed).

- CHEF PRIDE, Phase 2: rival chefs with a voice (developer 2026-11-23 "start building the next phase"; plan `docs/planning/CHEF-PRIDE-PLAN-2026-11-23.md` and the detailed PDF), UNCOMMITTED. `restaurant/rivalChefs.ts` (copy only, no save data): each of the 49 city rivals has a CHEF (name, a `dare` line while they are the next to pass, a `beaten` line when passed); 13 are FEATURED (spaced 3+ levels apart: Mama's Kitchen, Little Bistro, Green Fork, Harbor Grill, Copper Pan, Spice Route, Zen Garden, Golden Ladle, Hearth & Ember, Silver Birch, Velvet Spoon, Chez Margaux, Aurora). THE NAMES AND LINES ARE MY DRAFTS FOR THE DEVELOPER TO EDIT in that one file (QA only checks length, tone and uniqueness). Shown from the ranking news (Level 8): Restaurant Progress under "Next: pass ..." (`CityRankHero`, `rival-chef-line`), the Kitchen rank card on a window 700+ px tall (`kitchen-rival-chef`, hidden below, the card stays two lines), the celebration card when a rival is passed (the last passed rival's `beaten` line in the chef's hand, `celebration-rival`; App passes `rank.rival`; analytics `rival_passed {rank_to, passed}`) and, for FEATURED rivals only, one Paige message before the level that passes them (`assistant.ts` `rivalBeats`: id `rival:<id>`, two plain-English lines, priority 36 so it never outranks a teaching tip, once each via `seen`, only while that rival really is the next, Go there opens Progress). Paige's copy rule is ASCII only, so the three accented chef names are plain (Etienne, Nystrom, Amelie) and her quotes are straight. NOT built from the plan: the `respect` line kind (nothing to attach it to yet) and painted rival portraits (the rival's emoji is the face). Tests: new `scripts/rival-chefs-qa.mts` (cast of 49, tone: no insult / no population claim / no shouting / no repeats, Paige's beats: featured only, 3+ levels apart, gating, copy rule, celebration line, pure file, wiring) and `tools/e2e/rivals.mjs` (Progress line from Level 8 and none before, Kitchen card tall/short, Paige's two lines and Go there, marked as given and not back after a reload, fits at 320x568 / 390x844 / 1280x720): ALL PASS; assistant-qa, city-ranking, ui-copy, achievements, guide, save suites still pass. NOT seen in the browser: the celebration card with the rival line (a rank rise needs a real first completion; the line is covered by `rival-chefs-qa` D and the wiring checks); please look at it in a real service.

- CHEF PRIDE, Phases 0 + 1 (developer 2026-11-23: ego-driven recognition for Gen Z; the whole plan is `docs/planning/CHEF-PRIDE-PLAN-2026-11-23.md`, built in order, first release = Phases 1-3; the developer decided leagues are LOCAL vs the 49 city rivals). BUILT so far, UNCOMMITTED: `business.chefRecord?` (one optional field, read by `chefRecordOf`, `restaurant/chefRecord.ts`, pure: perfect cuts, longest combo, fastest plate, runs of Clean / Masterful plates, a service's accuracy against the last one and the best) written in the SAME save as the plate's result (`App.tsx` `noteRunStats` keeps the plate and `persist()` carries it, so no second stale save). `Preparation.tsx` gathers the plate (perfect cuts, cuts, an unpaused clock) and the Knife Report adds real lines: "★ Longest combo yet: 12", "★ Fastest plate yet: 14 s", "★ Most perfect cuts in a plate: N" only when a number STRICTLY beats a real earlier mark (a first plate sets marks, it is not a record), and a near-miss in WORDS ("A hair from Masterful. One more try?", within 4 points; Law 4 keeps digits off the report). After a service `completeCampaignLevel` records its average plate accuracy and perfect cuts and the Level Complete card (`MilestoneBanner` new `stats` prop) shows Orders / Accuracy / Perfects tiles and, when it beat the last service, "★ Accuracy up from 81% to 92%". No money, no ledger, no economy figure moved, no population claim (the game has no population data), analytics `record_broken {kind}`. Tests: new `scripts/chef-record-qa.mts` (A-G: clamping and old saves, plate and service maths, news is only a strict improvement, near-miss words only, rule file pure, no population claim / insult, wiring) and `tools/e2e/chefrecords.mjs` (Knife Report line at 390x844 and 1280x720, the plate lands in the save). NOT proven in the browser: the Level Complete card's tiles (a card needs a whole multi-order service the solver cannot finish reliably, and a one-order Level 1 rolls straight on and closes it): covered by `chef-record-qa` G4/G6 and a rendered screenshot of the card at 320, 390 and 1280 ("Perfect cuts" was shortened to "Perfects" because it truncated at 320). `GRADE_THRESHOLDS` in `CutEvaluator.ts` is now exported. STALE, not touched: `knifereport.mjs` times out at HEAD too (its fresh-player Level 1 never reaches the report). NEXT: Phase 2 (named rival chefs with lines), Phase 3 (titles and optional dares), then measure (Phase 7) before prestige, the local league and share cards.

- Landscape OVEN film, composed from two Gemini clips (developer 2026-11-23: "take necessary frames from these videos because Gemini is not properly generating it, and integrate the oven game properly"): `gemini_generated_video_9c596a30.mp4` keeps the loaf PALE for all 8 s and `gemini_generated_video_c5eadabb.mp4` has it DARK from the start, but both are the same shot (same camera push-in, flames and tray; only the loaf differs, pixel for pixel), so `scripts/make-oven-bread-wide-video.py` composes the bake: the pale clip is the picture, the loaf is found per frame (where the two clips differ strongly, biggest blob, filled, feathered), and inside it the dark clip is mixed in over the bake (pale, golden at 60-68 % = the golden window, deep brown, then burnt: darker and less saturated) with a little warmth so the mix does not go grey; flames, oven and tray are never mixed (no ghosting). Timeline = the oven step's: 1.5 s of the loaf carried in on the peel (source 0.7-2.2 s) then 6.5 s of bake (source 2.2-8.0 s slowed a little), 8.0 s, silent, 1280x720 (the source is 640x360: a Lanczos upscale with a light sharpen, so it is softer than the cooking films; no new detail is possible), the sparkle painted out of both clips first. Output `src/assets/video/oven-bread-wide.{mp4,webm}` (1.7 / 1.3 MB) and `-poster.webp`; dist 69 MiB. `OvenStep.tsx`: in a landscape window the film IS the picture (full frame, `object-position` 50 % 75 % so a very wide window crops the flames, not the tray), the title top left, the meter and "Take it out" in a panel at the bottom right (`oven-panel`, clamp(200px, 24vw, 320px), clear of the loaf); the film still follows the bake clock (golden at about 5.7 s) and no skip. The old portrait film in the middle of a painted oven picture is gone from the wide window (the picture `clip-oven-bread.webp` stays in the set, unused; an upright phone keeps the portrait film). RULE CHANGE in `landscape-art-qa` D7. New `tools/e2e/ovenwide.mjs` (1280x720, 1815x867, 740x360, 390x844: wide film, panel on screen with a 48 px button, golden at ~5.7 s, a double tap in the golden window, Knife Report, upright still portrait, no console errors): ALL PASS. STALE, not touched: `ovenstep.mjs` (and probably `ovenrecipes` / `ovenbaguette`) fail before the oven because their Level 5 seed is short of ingredients for the Pre-Service Check ("Restock to start"); `ovenwide.mjs` seeds the way `widefilm.mjs` does.

- Cooking films re-encoded sharper (developer 2026-11-22: "the chefs cooking videos quality is very low"): the first build scaled the 1280x720 sources down to 1024x576 at CRF 29 (MP4) / 36 (WebM), about 0.6 Mbps (SSIM against the source 0.961 on the bread film). `scripts/make-wide-cooking-videos.py` now keeps the source's 1280x720 and encodes at CRF 21 (H.264) / 28 (VP9), audio 96k / 64k, poster quality 88 (SSIM 0.97-0.99). All six wide films are 0.4-1.4 MB each way (was 0.2-0.5 MB); dist 62 -> 66 MiB, still far under the 200 MB zip limit, and the films load only when played. The limit now is the generated source itself (720p, 2-4 Mbps, with AI softness and steam over the chef's face in the curry film); a sharper film needs a higher-resolution generation. The portrait films (`chef-*.mp4`) have no sources in the repo, so they were not re-encoded.

- Landscape cooking films, all six kinds (developer 2026-11-22: "few of the cooking videos are generated, integrate it properly"): the curry, bread, cooking and plating films you generated (`Cooking Video Landscape/Chef *.mp4`, 1280x720 with sound, copied into each `NN-name/generated/NN-name_landscape.mp4`) are built by `scripts/make-wide-cooking-videos.py` (the sparkle painted out with `delogo`, same box for all four: checked on frames) into `src/assets/video/chef-{curry,bread,cooking,plating}-wide.{mp4,webm}` + posters and listed in `WIDE_CLIPS` (`CookingClip.tsx`), so in a wide window every cooking film plays full frame with SKIP in the bottom-right corner. Trims (the game never waits past 7 s): curry 10 s -> from 2.0 s at 1.5x = 5.4 s; plating 13.8 s -> its last 7.3 s at 1.4x = 5.3 s (the plate arriving, the herbs, the finished plate); bread 5.9 s and cooking 3.9 s whole. The oven's bread film (`07-oven-bread`) is NOT delivered: still portrait. Note: an oven dish plays the oven step instead of the cooking film, so the bread film shows only for a bread dish with no oven step (the peach-cheddar board in the campaign). Test: `widefilm.mjs` (RULE CHANGE: the curry no longer keeps the portrait film on its backdrop; new checks for bread, cooked, plated; the phone check is a flaky cutting solver, passes on rerun).

- Stirring sound (developer 2026-11-22: "while stirring add a satisfying stirring sound"): the Sauce Station's spoon and whisk run ONE looped recorded bed through `AudioManager.washStroke("stir" | "whisk", speed)` from the 50 ms tick (swells with the turning speed, also runs for the auto spoon); `wash-stir` = water swirl + spoon-on-bowl friction + soft gloop, `wash-whisk` = brighter splashy bed (built in `make-audio-assets.py`). The old `sauceWhisk` swish per 240 ms is no longer played (kept in the SFX table). Not heard by me. `audio-qa` Z7 (RULE CHANGE) / Z11.

- Cutting sounds (developer 2026-11-22: "realistic cutting sounds on a wooden board, different for different ingredients"): `playIngredientSlice` now plays a RECORDED knife cut of the family that belongs to the food (`src/game/audio/cutSounds.ts`: crunch, juicy, leaf, bulb, bread, melon, citrus, mushroom, cheese, meat, dense = the old pumpkin cuts), a different recording each time, plus a real `boardtap` for the stroke families. 31 clips + the tap from 20 Freesound CC0 recordings (preview files, `fetch-audio-sources.py`, credits in `docs/AUDIO-CREDITS.md`). NOT heard by me (picked by spectrogram and levels): the developer should listen and name the foods that sound wrong (each clip is one line in `CUTS` in `make-audio-assets.py`). `audio-qa` Z10.

## Mobile and mobile landscape sweep (developer 2026-11-18: "optimize the game for mobile and mobile landscape orientation")

- **New measuring tool:** `tools/e2e/phoneaudit.mjs` visits 16 screens (Kitchen, the Mini-games sheet, all eight games, Market, restaurant map, Business, Inventory, Progress, Settings) at nine phone sizes (320x568, 360x640, 390x844, 430x932 upright; 568x320, 640x360, 740x360, 844x390, 932x430 on its side), with each how-to card open and then closed, and measures sideways scroll, controls cut off or unreachable, controls under 48 px and text under 8.5 px. `--shots` saves 844x390 and 390x844 pictures, `--only=car-park,market` picks screens. Guard: `scripts/phone-layout-qa.mts`.
- **Fixed (found by it):** every how-to / pause / result card of the games ran off a short landscape screen and its Got it / Next button could not be reached (15 overlays: they centred with a grid that cannot cap the card's height; now they scroll with the overlay and centre with `m-auto`, and the plain how-tos lay out in two or three columns on their side). The **Sauce Station** had its pantry (the pour buttons) clipped off the bottom in landscape: it now mixes in two columns (bowl and gauges left; steps, pantry and buttons right; `display: contents` groups plus `order-*` keep the upright layout exactly as it was). **Fridge Sort** (basket left, shelves right) and the **Car Park** (the lot as tall as the screen, 12 px cells became 33 px at 568x320) got landscape layouts. The Kitchen's card row swipes sideways on a short landscape screen (City Ranking was squeezed to 28 px). View Details, the map's zoom buttons and the Car Park's Let's go are 48 px.
- **Tests:** `landscape.mjs` check 2 now counts a control inside a horizontally scrolling row as reachable (rule change: the Kitchen row swipes). Its checks 4a, 4b and 6 (the cutting canvas) were already failing before this work (stale seeds, see below). The disk filled up once from the browser tests' throwaway Chrome profiles (`tools/e2e/profile-*`, 11 GB; they are git-ignored and safe to delete).

Resting and locked are now made from the ready frame (`scripts/make-minigame-assets.py` `recolor_ready`: dusty brown / dark walnut, same 288 x 288 slice 72), and the icon sits inside the frame (`mini-games-qa` E2b).
- **Dessert Run: six steps and eight pictures (developer 2026-11-19: "plan the levels easy to hard as they level up, and make the mosaic beautiful", UNCOMMITTED):** measured first: only four pictures served Levels 20-91 (Level 50-91 showed the cupcake / donut over and over), four difficulty steps, and a +/-4 random jitter that put some levels below the one before. Now `RUN_TIERS` has six steps (Levels 18 / 30 / 42 / 54 / 66 / 78; lanes 3 / 3 / 4 / 4 / 5 / 5; waiters carry fewer cubes each step), `RUN_HARDNESS` windows rise and meet (0-.06 / .04-.15 / .10-.24 / .18-.33 / .27-.42 / .36-.55), the jitter is +/-2. Four new pictures in `dessertGallery.ts`: Macaron Tower (pink + lilac on mint), Maple Pancakes, Strawberry Chocolate Cake, Rainbow Lollipop (a spiral, 9 colour groups); every picture is clipped to the plate well (`Painter.result`), so each holds exactly 568 cubes. Rosters: T1 cookie, macaron, donut; T2 donut, cupcake, pancakes; T3 pancakes, cone, cupcake; T4 cake, cone, pancakes; T5 cake, lollipop, macaron; T6 lollipop, cake, macaron (the cupcake is a very easy picture, so it sits in the middle steps). Plates re-proven and re-rated: 18 x 60 in `dessertSeeds.ts` (`make-dessert-seeds.mts`; the easy pictures need 800-1500 candidates for the harder windows, the pool is the git-ignored `scripts/.dessert-pool/`). Measured careful-player hardness by step 0.01 / 0.10 / 0.15 / 0.23 / 0.32 / 0.45; careless wins 78 / 60 / 28 / 13 / 3 / 0 %. Changing a picture's CUBES (not only a tone colour) invalidates its pool: re-rate it. `dessert-run-qa` updated to the new rule (RULE CHANGE: six tiers, eight pictures, 568 each); `dessertrun.mjs` D6 now runs Levels 18 / 54 / 91, and D4 compares Try again with the plate that jammed (a won round moves on first). Not played by hand.
- **Unload the Van grows with the level (developer 2026-11-19: "gradually add more crates, more rows and columns; all levels feel the same", UNCOMMITTED):** measured first: Levels 62-91 were one identical 7 x 7 board (11-12 crates), the grid changed size only at Levels 28 and 45, the ramp mixed a tier's crate count with the old grid (a 5 x 5 board at 79 % full). Now `CRATE_TIERS` has thirteen steps (Levels 18, 23, 28, 34, 40, 46, 52, 58, 66, 72, 78, 84, 91): grid 5 x 5 (L18) -> 6 x 6 (L28) -> 7 x 7 (L46) -> 8 x 8 (L66; never more: a cell is 36 px on a 390 px phone), crates 5-6 -> 14-16, dark blocks 2-3 -> 9-10, doors 3 -> 8, waves 3 -> 9, free-at-start 4 -> 3, a 3 x 3 crate from Level 66; a board is never more than about two thirds full. `crateBoardForTier` tries 240 / 400 / 600 builds (6 / 7 / 8 wide) and keeps the board nearest the tier's numbers if none meets them (about 1 board in 60 on 8 x 8 needs more than 240). `crate-dispatch-qa` A1 / A7 / A8 / A9 and C1 were updated to the new rule (RULE CHANGE, said in the QA); `kitchendispatch.mjs` K9 now runs Levels 18, 46 and 91; the clock rule (40 s + 9 s a crate) is unchanged. - **Painted icons INTEGRATED (developer 2026-11-21: the 10 Codex icon sheets are delivered, "start integrating", uncommitted):** `scripts/make-icon-assets.py` (new) cuts the sheets in `Icon Sheets Assets/` (git-ignored, 5 x 5 cells of 400 px, awards-nature 6 x 5) into `public/icons/<slug>.webp` (128 px, 254 icons, 2.2 MB) and writes `src/lib/paintedIconFiles.ts` (emoji -> slug). An icon is cut by its SHAPES (connected pixels, a small dilation joins a sparkle to its icon, each shape goes to the cell of its centre; a shape bigger than 1.35 cells is two icons touching and is split on the grid line, none were), not by the grid line, because several icons spill a few pixels over their cell. 👵 and 👵🏻 are one key (the neutral grandma wins; `grandma-light` was not kept). `paintedIcons.ts`: `ICON_FILES = PAINTED_ICON_FILES`; an ingredient's own painting still wins (the Market and the recipes show the same picture), then the sheet icon, then the coloured line icon (now only for an emoji the game never uses). `.gi.gi-pic` in `styles.css`: 1.4 em, `vertical-align: middle`, `margin-block: -0.25em` (the first values sat the pictures too low: an inline-block's baseline is its strut's, not its bottom). RULE CHANGES (tests updated): `emoji-icons-qa` B2 (the "emoji with no icon" example is the evil eye, the olive is painted now), new F1-F4 (every mapped emoji has its file and none is orphaned, each under 24 KB, the icons of the screenshots are painted, plain keys), `emojiicons.mjs` check 4 and the probe (`.gi-img` counts as a drawn icon, a painted one is 1.4-1.45 em, size window 6-48 px). epaint\`) and that prompt file and its colour script were removed. The real complaint: the colour is fine but the current maps look too sharpened, not smooth or realistic, unlike the concept. Measured: Codex source `TL-restaurant-06-grand.png` has about 3-4x the micro-contrast (Laplacian variance 6289 vs about 1750) and about 4x the edge halos (10.7 % vs 2.7 %) of the portrait concept; the game's webp (6062, 10.4 %) is slightly softer than the source, so OUR pipeline does not sharpen. `docs/planning/MAPS-QUALITY-SMOOTHING-CODEX-PROMPTS-2026-11-21.md`: one prompt (attach the concept AND the current picture; keep colour and layout locked; no sharpening look, smooth surfaces, calm small details, soft light, render at 3840 x 2160 and reduce without sharpening) for the 6 maps and the Second Floor, saved to `true-landscape\smooth\`. NEW `scripts/check-map-quality.py <new> [old]`: colour unchanged, layout unchanged, fine-detail energy 35-75 % of the old, halos at most 70 % (calibrated: a 0.9 px blur scores 40 %; the unchanged pictures fail). Offered, NOT done: a stopgap soften in `make-landscape-assets.py`; the same check for the Kitchen and mini-game rooms.
- **Delivery Inspection: pixel-accurate taps; Fridge Sort: the whole basket on the cloth (developer 2026-11-21: "I clicked the sweet potato behind and it picked the wrong item, optimize the touch and its accuracy"; "place the basket properly on the table cloth and make it perfect", uncommitted):** (1) TAPS: every ingredient is a round box and a box drawn later took the tap even where its picture is empty, so a tap on a half-hidden sweet potato went to the zucchini in front. New `inspectionHit.ts`: the tap is judged on each picture's own solid pixels (alpha read once into a small canvas per ingredient, `loadMask`), the box turned by its tilt, topmost first (`zOf` paint order), and a fingertip of slack (`SLACK_PX` 14 px, rings of 3 / 6 / 10 / 14 px, the first ring with a solid pixel decides). `DeliveryInspectionGame.tsx`: `pick()` in `onDown` chooses the item (found and gone pieces are skipped, so a tap goes through a stamped piece to what lay behind it), `onUp` flags the PICKED item (`flag(d.id)`), drags use the picked item too; the DOM events are still on the item boxes (keyboard and tests unchanged). Guards: `scripts/inspection-hit-qa.mts` A-E (new) and `deliveryinspection.mjs` I15 (new: finds a real point on a real crate where an ingredient behind is covered only by an empty box and taps it with a real mouse; it must go to the one behind). Left as they are, to be re-aimed by the developer's rules, not weakened. **v1.6.0 uploaded to Playgama (2026-11-19, not live):** archive `knifecraft-playgama-1.6.0b` (PASSED; made with Python `zipfile`, because a PowerShell `Compress-Archive` zip of the same files was refused) is in the form with the category `management` and the corrected description; the live catalog is still 1.4.1. Going live = `send_playgama_catalog_update` (no moderation, needs the developer's yes and an update note). The five compliance fixes are in `docs/PLATFORM-COMPLIANCE-2026-11-19.md`. **Windows are always filled and emoji are icons since 2026-11-19 (compliance fixes 3 and 4 of 5):** `fitLayout` / `GameShell` fill every window edge to edge (CLAUDE.md section 1); `Phaser` measures its layout box (`GameBridge`); `StageSides` / `FloorSides` carry the rooms sideways; no blurred side area anywhere. The emoji of the screens and data are drawn as Lucide icons by `src/lib/emoji-jsx` (the build's JSX runtime) from `src/lib/emojiIcons.ts` (88 %, `emoji-icons-qa`); the character stays as inline zero-size text so `innerText` reads as before; unmapped food pictures stay emoji until painted art exists. Do NOT run `prettier --write` on whole folders (it reformatted files that were not meant to change). **Interstitials follow the Bridge since 2026-11-19 (compliance fix 2 of 5):** the game keeps no level counter and no cooldown (`interstitialPolicy.ts` only guards duplicates / an ad in the way / no ads API); `playgama-bridge-config.json` has no `minimumDelayBetweenInterstitial` any more (platform default); the first-clear "+50%" offer always follows the interstitial request. **Sound is recorded since 2026-11-19 (compliance fix 1 of 5):** `public/audio` (38 CC0 mp3s, 4.3 MB; `scripts/fetch-audio-sources.py` -> `scripts/make-audio-assets.py` -> `src/game/audio/audioFiles.ts`; credits `docs/AUDIO-CREDITS.md`); `AudioManager.ts` plays files only (tonal recordings are voices played at any note by playback rate; the knife cuts are real recordings shaped by each ingredient's audio profile); four recorded music loops crossfade by mood; `relaxComposer.ts` and every oscillator are deleted; `audio-qa` / `music-qa` rewritten (RULE CHANGE, documented in their headers), preflight fails on `createOscillator`. NOT HEARD BY EAR: the developer must listen on a phone and say which sounds to change. **Platform compliance audit (developer 2026-11-19: "check every rule of Playgama and YouTube Playables"):** full list in `docs/PLATFORM-COMPLIANCE-2026-11-19.md`. FAILING and waiting for the developer: all sound is synthesized in code (Playgama 3.3.2 wants recorded or tool-made ogg / mp3), our own interstitial gate (min 3 levels, 150 s) against Bridge Required step 7, empty bars in every window that is not an upright phone (1.6.1 / 1.6.8, YouTube, MSN), emoji used as icons (4.3.2), and the store form (no category, text still says 250 levels / 25 chapters, live build v1.4.1). FIXED the same day: no `sessionStorage` / cookie (3.2.1), no Esc handling (1.5.2, preflight guards both), no share prompt on YouTube (Design 6.1). **Every mini-game climbs level by level (developer 2026-11-19: "make sure the levels match the rest of the game, all mini-games"):** the rule of 2026-11-11 (`difficultyRamp.ts`: numbers move from one tier's value to the next tier's, equal to the tier's own on its first level, never easier as the level rises) already held for Fridge Sort, Stock the shelves, the delivery check and Fridge Check; the audit found the other three flat for 10-33 levels (Sauce Station Levels 7-39 one 90 s round; Car Park 22-31 one 8-10 vehicle lot; the Van 18-27 one 5-6 crate board). Now `sauceStation.tierAt` ramps the bowl, the clock (whole seconds), the colour tolerance, the oil limit, the Taste looks (only between finite tiers), the bottles and the fill window; `carPark.carParkTierAt` ramps the vehicles, the free ones and the chain; `crateDispatch.crateTierAt` ramps the crates, the dark blocks, the ingredients (capped by the doors the grid has room for, `gateCapacity`: the 6 x 6 board shows 4), the waves and the free crates. Boards, lots and tiers keep their size, shapes and door width. Dessert Run climbs through its rated plate ramp (next entry). `difficulty-ramp-qa` S1-S3 / P1-P3, `crate-dispatch-qa` A9 (every level 18-91, 25 boards each) and `car-park-qa` A10 / A11 (every level 15-91) hold it; `carpark.mjs` now waits for the loading screen to clear before clicking (a stale 300 ms timing). **Dessert Run levels rated and ramped (developer 2026-11-19: "ensure and increase the quality of the levels"):** every plate is rated by a careful-player bot (`ratePlate` / `playCareful`, 12 rounds; `hardness` 0 = always won with room to spare), `RUN_HARDNESS` gives each tier a window (0-0.1 / 0.02-0.25 / 0.06-0.35 / 0.12-0.5), and `make-dessert-seeds.mts` keeps 60 proven plates per picture inside it, easiest first (rating cache in the git-ignored `scripts/.dessert-pool/`; cupcake needs ~1100 candidates to fill the harder windows). `dessertRunFor` puts a level on its tier's ramp (first level = gentle plates, last level = the tier's hardest; day and round move it +/- 4 seeds). Tier 4 now also deals the cone (cone, cupcake, donut). Measured: careful-player hardness by tier 0.03 / 0.10 / 0.17 / 0.29, careless wins 85 / 68 / 35 / 15 %. Before: the picture decided difficulty (the Level 65+ cupcake was easier than the Level 28 donut). `dessert-run-qa` F2 and G hold it. The colour highlight is OFF for good (developer 2026-11-19: pressing or hovering a waiter used to light up its colour on the mosaic and dim the rest; the `peek` view state is deleted, `dessert-run-qa` I guards it). Guests are drawn 95 px (105), the running waiter 72 px (62) and its cube rides on the tray; the served cubes lie inside each guest plate's well, middle first (`plateLayout`), instead of stacking up past the rim (developer 2026-11-19). Guests are drawn 95 px (105), the running waiter 72 px (62) and its cube rides on the tray; the served cubes lie inside each guest plate's well, middle first (`plateLayout`), instead of stacking up past the rim (developer 2026-11-19). The cookie (bite, chunky chips), donut (drippy icing, sprinkles) and cone (big scoop, cherry, waffle) are repainted the same way, each filling the round well (developer 2026-11-18). The cupcake is repainted (cherry with stem and leaf, three frosting swirls with cream ribbons, sprinkles, striped cup; a seventh colour group, the leaf green; new tones `redLight`, `leaf`, `leafDark`). All 480 plates were re-proven (`make-dessert-seeds.mts`; `dessertSeeds.ts` regenerated). `dessert-run-qa` updated to the new rule: 568 cubes each, and the board is exactly the cubes that meet the clip circle (none missing at the rim, none hidden outside it). The cookie, donut and cone pictures are unchanged apart from the wider board; they have NOT been repainted to match the cupcake.
- **Mini-games sheet tightened (developer 2026-11-18, UNCOMMITTED):** smaller title block, short-screen tiles (`max-height:700px`: min-h 116, icon 64), landscape = four columns (all eight games on one screen at 844x390; icon 54, tile min-h 104), the sheet up to 780 px wide on its side, a fade at the list's bottom edge, status pills sized from the tile's width (`cqw`, 9-11.5 px). Status copy shortened so it fits its painted pill: "Ad: 3 more rounds" (was "More rounds for an ad"), "Back tomorrow" (was "Done / Cleared · back tomorrow"; `mini-games-qa` expects the new text). At 568x320 two pills still wrap to two lines inside their frame.
- **Decided (developer 2026-11-18):** **Unload the Van** and **Dessert Run** are fixed portrait stages (a strip about 220 px wide, text 4-7 px, on a phone on its side), so on its side each shows a "Turn your phone upright" card with "Play anyway" and Leave (`common/TurnUprightCard.tsx`, `useTurnUpright.ts`; the round / clock waits behind the card; turning the phone upright lifts it; `phone-layout-qa` D1/D2, `phoneaudit.mjs` measures the card and clicks Play anyway). Played with "Play anyway" the stage is still small; a real landscape redraw of both stages is not planned. Fridge Check's item card label ("4 lb", "Onion") also scales down to 1-6 px in landscape (it shares the card scaling).

## Image sizes trimmed (developer 2026-11-18: "reduce all the game assets size to the maximum, without ruining the quality")

- `python scripts/optimize-images.py` (dry run; `--apply` writes) re-encodes every WebP in `public/` and `src/assets/` at the lowest quality that still passes SSIM against the file as it is (colour >= 0.99, worst 1 % of the picture >= 0.95, cut-out alpha >= 0.995 and no alpha step over 40). A file that does not shrink 4 % stays untouched. 494 of 820 files shrank: 23.10 MB -> 21.12 MB (-8.6 %), almost all cut-out sprites (soft alpha); the big opaque backgrounds were already at their floor. Checked side by side (people, dessert run, sauce bottles, plate): no visible change.
- Not changed on purpose: dimensions (set by `scripts/make-*.py`, 3x the biggest on-screen size), the cooking clips (each player downloads one of mp4 / webm, 0.3-0.6 MB), the JS (3.65 MB, a code-split question, not an asset one). Run the script again after any new art: it costs about 25 minutes for the whole tree.

## Deep check of the whole game (developer 2026-11-18): what was found and fixed
Method: every QA suite run, three read-only audits (new code of the last days; money / saves / determinism; unbuilt and unfinished parts), each finding verified in the code before it was changed.
**Fixed (guards: save-safety-qa, audit-2026-11-18-qa, sauce-station-qa T7/U, crate-dispatch-qa D4, loading-screen-qa B4/D6/D7):**
- **Save wipe (the most serious):** a Bridge storage read that failed was treated as "no save" and the first write then replaced the real save with a new game. `SaveManager.load` now retries three times; if the read still fails the session plays on defaults in memory and `save()` writes NOTHING over the real save. An unreadable / malformed save is copied to `knifecraft_save_backup` before the defaults replace it (`saveCloudBackup`, PlayablesSDK). A stored wallet that is negative, fractional or not a number is made whole cents >= 0 at load (it made `walletInvariantViolation` refuse every later write).
- **Daily counters before Level 10:** `business.calendar.businessDay` only advances from Level 10, so the Sauce Station's 3 rounds a day (opens at Level 7), the delivery check, the Car Park's lot / daily flag and the step-skip counter never reset at Levels 3-9. They now read `playDayOf(save)` (`business/playDay.ts`: the larger of the food calendar and `restaurantDay.day`). Food freshness keeps its own calendar.
- **Loading screen:** it now swallows taps while it is up (the game could be tapped unseen under it; the e2e harness makes it click-through), has an inline 25 s watchdog in index.html ("tap to reload" if the game's code never arrives), the failsafe is armed before the platform starts, a throw while the save is applied clears it, it waits for the Kitchen's code (`loadScreensRouter`) so it does not fade into a second loading screen, and "tap for music" shows only to a player with sound and music on.
- **Car Park:** the lot was rebuilt on every `save` change (a hitch of up to a few hundred ms on a hard tier, and "Try again" could land on another lot). Built once per visit now. This probably also fixes the old e2e carpark P6 failure (it passes now).
- **Mini-games sheet:** the glow and star were clipped by the list; the art probe retries a file that failed once (8 s) and no longer misses a late result; focus is trapped in the dialog and returns on close; tiles in a row share a height; resting lines say "back tomorrow" (Sauce, Car Park).
- **Sauce Station:** a zero-star round on a sauce already on the shelf said "Not on the shelf"; nothing is finished or paid if the player leaves during the pause after a bottle.
- **Unload the Van:** an Undo that can no longer be done is dropped and the older one is tried (it was a dead button that blocked every older Undo).
- **Copy / touch rules:** the free-play lock notice named a level ("opens at Level 18"): now "in N services". Eight buttons under 48 px raised (the "?" on Check the delivery / Fridge Sort / Stockroom / Fridge Check, the pre-service dismiss, the moment chip's close, three maintenance buttons, the Fridge Check how-to link). The Stockroom hint explains itself when tapped early. Stale comments corrected (first levels, "no music", orphan doc comment). The Market's buy handlers start from the latest save.
**Checked and found clean (audits):** every `credits` change writes exactly one ledger entry (or none) and cannot go below 0; rewarded-ad grants only on `rewarded` with their caps; bitmasks stay under 31 bits (24 sauces); Math.random is cosmetic only in the restaurant build; every ScreenId has a route.
**Known, NOT changed (decisions for the developer):**
- **Device clock:** the daily gift and its streak, the welcome-back offer, every ad cap and the Second Floor's collections use the phone's own date: moving the clock forward earns another gift / cap / collection. It cannot be closed without a server clock; a partial guard (refuse claims when the clock is behind the newest ledger entry) only stops moving it BACK.
- The rush-restock and service-cover rewarded ads have no per-day count; they are limited by need (what the order or service is short of). CLAUDE.md section 2 says each ad has a cap: say if you want a count added (it would also cut ad revenue).
- Other money handlers in App.tsx (`buyKnife`, `buyBoard`, `sharpenKnife`, `upgradeKnife`, `buyStaff`, `hireDayCleanerPaid`, refrigerator / supplier actions, `advanceBusinessDay`, the serve and result recorders) still build on the render's `save` (a persist in the same tick would be undone; narrow). The Replay Bonus's read-back could roll back a write that lands during its awaits. A second rewarded ad can open if the first never answered within 30 s (it then pays nothing).
- `?dessertRun=` / `?kitchenDispatch` skip the level gate (the documented preview links). The Dessert Run "Tap me" hint always points at lane 0.
- ~60 exported helpers are used nowhere (dead code, harmless); `qualityModifierFor` is an unused forward hook.
- Stale browser tests (their testids no longer exist): idle, restaurantsecondfloor, tablecash, cashroom, dining, phase2, restaurantgrandmasfridge (and possibly parts of restaurantname / deliveryinspection guide ids); the dishwashing e2e. Riskiest thin coverage: interstitialPolicy and the real Bridge path, the Car Park tow ad, skip_step, kitchenMoments, discardExpired in a browser.
- Suites that diff against HEAD (phase2-6, next-kitchen-strip, oven-step E1, service-floor) fail on a dirty tree because SaveManager changed; they pass once it is committed. oven-step E2 and restaurant-measures A4 are old stale checks. There is no store text in `docs/playgama`, so the rule "store text updated in the same release" cannot be checked from the repo.

## Loading screen (developer 2026-11-18)
**Why:** measured on Slow 4G (1.6 Mbps, 150 ms) the page was WHITE for about 6 s, then a plain cream "warming the kitchen" screen, then the Kitchen's art painted in piece by piece (a returning player's backdrop and Paige arrived ~15 s in); a new player saw cream placeholders where the story pictures were still arriving. Fast 4G: first content at ~3.4 s.
**What:** `index.html` now paints a loading screen by itself (inline CSS + a 383-character blurred placeholder, logo and bar frame preloaded at high priority, big background/chef/board at low priority) before any script arrives; `src/lib/bootSplash.ts` fills its bar with real progress (code arrived 30 %, save loaded 50 %, then the first screen's pictures and fonts) and removes it; `App.tsx` waits (max 9 s, hard limit 20 s) for the equipped kitchen's backdrop and Paige's cutout (a new player: the first two story pictures); `main.tsx` clears it on a render failure. The element never blocks taps (`pointer-events: none`), keeps a 9:16 shape on wide windows, and the "tap for music" hint sits on the bar's plank. Pictures: `scripts/make-loading-assets.py` turns the Codex files in `Loading Screen Assets\` (background, logo, chef cutout, board, bar frame; the "Concept" picture has words painted in and is NOT used) into 311 KB of WebP in `public/loading/` (the source folder stays untracked, like the other Codex folders).
**After:** on Slow 4G the placeholder is on screen under a second in, the logo at ~2 s, the full picture at ~5 s, and the game appears whole (backdrop already loaded). Tests: `scripts/loading-screen-qa.mts` (A-D), `tools/e2e/loadingscreen.mjs` (L1-L7, throttled, new and returning player, three sizes); the e2e harness `boot()` now waits for the screen to leave. Preflight passes. The in-app `LoadingScreen` (the cream "warming the kitchen" between screens) is unchanged.
**Not done / ideas:** the chef cutout's edge fringe is only softened; a progress label per step ("Warming the oven...") would need copy; the rest of the Kitchen's pictures (shortcut icons, banners) still load as before, after the screen leaves.

## Sauce Shelf did not grow after two sauces (developer 2026-11-17, UNCOMMITTED)
Cause found in the code (not reproduced on the developer's save): `sauceOfTheDay` skipped only MASTERED (3-star) sauces, so a sauce made at 1-2 stars was dealt again and again and the shelf stayed at 1/24; and the result screen only said "On your Sauce Shelf!" at 3 stars, never for a new 1-2 star sauce, a 0-star round (needs 1 star) or an extra round (never shelved). Fix: the day's sauce prefers one NOT MADE, then not mastered, then any; `RoundEffects.shelved`; the result always says "Added to your Sauce Shelf!" / "Already on your Sauce Shelf" / "Not on the shelf: it needs 1 star" / extra rounds "not added to the shelf"; the old "On your Sauce Shelf!" line (testid `sauce-mastered`) is now `sauce-shelf-note`. Tests: sauce-station-qa T1-T6. If the shelf still misses a sauce, the developer's save is the next place to look (practice rounds and 0-star rounds never shelve).

Reported: Guacamole showed tomato and onion pieces floating while only avocado was poured; the Watch-an-ad button did not say what it is for. Fixed and then audited every mini-game for the same classes (a picture/tick/cue built from the answer, not from the player's action; a button that does not say what it does; instructions hidden at a hard tier; state bugs; wasted renders).
- **Sauce Station:** `bowlChunks(sauce, scoops)` (sauceStation.ts): a piece floats only for an ingredient really poured (1-3 by amount). The ad button is explained ("An ad gives 3 more to cook, not a skip"). An ingredient poured past its count (or not in the recipe) shows "over" / "extra" and the step says "Too much X: tap undo", never a tick or "Looks good!". "LET GO NOW!" lights only where the release scores well (`BOTTLE_GOOD`), "Too much!" past it. "Bottle it" on an empty bowl says to pour first and an empty bowl can never earn stars. The wax seal no longer leaks the live match on limited-taste tiers. Undo takes an oil scoop out of the "too fast" count. "Oil in slowly" and "Taste ↓" are visible words. "Play again · just for fun", "Next sauce · N left", "Prep Cook does it · no stars" (also in the three sort/inspection games). The clock redraws once a second. Chips go two-a-row on short screens.

- **Unload the Van:** the 650 ms win delay re-checks the board, so Undo inside it can no longer give "All dispatched!" with a crate left. Banner shortened (ui-copy W1 had failed on my own 15-word sentence). **Check the delivery:** a tap after the last defect no longer counts a wrong; the 12 s hint ring also shows under Reduced Motion. **Car Park:** "Tow used" (the tow comes back on Try again; e2e carpark.mjs regex updated).
- Tests: sauce-station-qa Q (chunks), R (cues, over-pour, empty bowl, seal, hints, labels), M3 updated to the new cue rule; saucestation.mjs S3d. carpark.mjs P6 (Tow takes one vehicle) fails with before=9 after=9, not re-run at HEAD (not a label problem).
- **Second pass (same day, the "found but not fixed" list):** Check the delivery's how-to now opens on the first crate of each NEW tier (`howto` bit 1 = seen, bits above = highest tier shown; `howtoDue`; old saves see their tier's guide once; delivery-inspection-qa A14/A15, e2e I2 reads `howto & 1`). Fridge Check's pause-card skip is the developer's own design (2026-11-06: does the chore, no stars; e2e T6), so it stays but is labelled "Skip it: chore done, no stars" in the cleaning flow (`skipMarksDone`). Dishwashing: Closing Time keeps ONE `SinkWash` mounted for the row's life (`showButton`), the soap is checked before the NEXT dish is shown (`soapEmpty`), the combo chip ends after its window, timers/rAF clear on close under Reduced Motion (dish-washing-qa X1-X4 are source checks; the dishwashing e2e are still stale, so this was NOT driven in a browser). Unload the Van repaints only while something moves (a still board 4 times a second) and the room+floor is a cached layer; Dessert Run repaints at about 10 fps while paused or over (not measured, logic only). Sauce Station at 320x568: pantry in two shelves (six 48 px tiles, narrower margins), recipe chips two a row.
- **Still open:** Sauce: pausing during the 700 ms bottle-capped timer lets finish run after "Leave"; Kitchen Dispatch: an undo that no longer fits blocks older undos; Dessert Run: the "Tap me" hint always points at lane 0; Fridge Sort: shelves with need 0 are dimmed (probably intended teaching); Stockroom's icon-only Hint button has no caption; car-park-qa A65t is a timing check that flakes under load; e2e carpark P6 fails (not re-run at HEAD).

The 24 foods sit in 8 colour groups by their real colour (`CRATE_FOODS`: red tomato/chilli/paprika, orange carrot/mango/turmeric, green lettuce/basil/parsley, teal broccoli/avocado/olive oil, purple onion/garlic/ginger, yellow cheddar/corn/lemon, blue salmon/yogurt/milk, brown mushroom/potato/cumin); foods of one group never meet on a board, and a board picks one of the three by level and seed (8 painted for this game, 16 reused from the Sauce Station's `products/`). The rules stay colour-key to colour-key, so every board is still won by construction; `crate-dispatch-qa` Z1-Z3 hold the new rule.

Fridge Sort and Stock the shelves already sort, so a colour-sorting game would overlap them.

## Sauce Station shows the recipe and the steps on EVERY tier (developer 2026-11-17, UNCOMMITTED)

Third report ("why is the sauce station still not showing proper steps, make sure this never repeats"): at Level 55+ (tiers 3 and 4) the game hid the ingredient list on purpose, so the screen said only "Tap the ingredients to pour them in" and the player had to guess the ingredients from a colour among ~16 pantry items. The 2026-11-16 fix had added the Pour / Blend / Bottle ladder but left the list "a tier's choice"; that was the gap.

- **Rule now:** the recipe is never a difficulty. `SauceTier.help` is gone; every tier shows a chip per ingredient (icon, name, `0/3` up to a tick, "pinch" for a pinch), the wanted pantry tiles glow, and the line under the ladder says `Pour: Olive oil ×3 · Lemon ×2 · Water ×1 …`. A harder tier is still a shorter clock, tighter colour tolerance and oil limit, narrower bottle window and fewer Taste looks.
- **Never again:** `sauce-station-qa` L9 / L9b / L9c (no `help` switch in `SAUCE_TIERS`, no `tier.help` / `showsRecipe` in the screen, the chips and the Pour line are drawn unconditionally, every sauce has a recipe of pantry items); e2e `saucestation.mjs` S3c at Levels 7 / 40 / 55 / 75 on a 320 x 568 phone (a chip per recipe item, those tiles glow, the step says Pour:, nothing sideways); new rule in CLAUDE.md §10 ("Mini-game instructions are never a difficulty").
- **Checked** by screenshot at 390 x 844 and 320 x 568 (Level 75, the hardest tier).

Now `dishDirt.ts` has `DISH_CENTER` (each piece's middle) and `DISH_BASIN_CENTER` (the water's middle, scene 180,140; the water is about 205 scene units tall, measured on a phone screenshot); `WashUpSink.tsx` moves the piece's middle onto the basin's middle while it zooms. New zooms: glass 1.4 (was 1.5), fork/knife/spoon 1.28 (was 1.7), utensils 1.3 (was 1.6); the rest are unchanged. The sponge waits at 54%/54% of the scene (on the piece). ## Buy all left ingredients "needed" (expired lots), and the "game hit a snag" screen (developer 2026-11-17)

Player report from a phone: "Open for service" showed "The game hit a snag" and Reload did nothing three times; later the game opened but still showed 2 ingredients needed after "Buy all" for 3 days, and buying them one by one fixed it.

- **Cause of the "needed" bug (reproduced):** a stock entry has ONE weighted-average age (`addStock`). A purchase on top of an EXPIRED lot of the same food was averaged into it, so the whole lot stayed expired (on a shortage day a big line is bought in parts of 10 and the rounding kept the old day for all six parts). `scripts` probe: 7 of 3,738 plan runs left the next service short; 0 now. Expired lots stay in the fridge until the night's sweep or "Throw Out Expired", so any morning after a 3-day protein can hit it.
- **Fix (rule, not a test change):** every purchase clears the expired stock of the ingredients it adds first: `discardExpired.ts` `withoutExpiredOf(save, ids)` (the same sweep as Throw Out Expired, optional ingredient list; waste recorded in today's `discardedQuantity/Value` and the lifetime totals, no money, no ledger). Used by the Market purchase (`purchaseIngredient`, which also quotes on the cleared save so the room the expired lot took is free), the rush restock and the quick restock. The two Service Covers already did this. `business-rush-restock-qa` D4 changed to the new rule (the restock now goes through and the old lot is waste; it used to refuse with `wouldNotUnblock`, which stays as a safety net); `assistant-qa` H7 changed to the 2026-11-17 Paige scroll rule (overflow-y auto, x hidden, no scrollbar).
- **The snag screen:** the most likely cause is a phone holding the previous index.html right after a deploy (every release renames the lazy chunks; the host lets browsers keep index.html for minutes and a plain reload can be answered from it). `src/lib/freshReload.ts`: a chunk-load error (error boundary or Vite's `vite:preloadError`) reloads the page once a minute under a new `?fresh=<time>` query string, and the screen's "Reload game" button always does; the save is in Bridge storage so nothing is lost. Not proved on a real phone (it could also have been a genuine render error; the console error would say).
- **Tests:** `scripts/expired-restock-qa.mts` (A the rule, B Market plan -> Buy all -> next service ready in 3,738 cases with expired lots in the fridge, C quick restock, D the snag screen), e2e `tools/e2e/chunkreload.mjs` R1-R5 (the chunk blocked: fresh load, no loop, button recovers, save untouched).
- **Mini-games sheet wording:** a locked tile said "Level 18"; it now says "opens in N services" (restaurant-build rule, `service-loop-qa` U1; `tools/e2e/minigames.mjs` M2 updated).
- **Known failures NOT from this work (identical at the previous commit):** `restaurant-measures-qa` A4 (looks for a `max-w-[60%]` class removed in 7244ca4, stale); `car-park-qa` A32t/A45t/A65t (lot-build timing on this machine). `oven-step-qa` E2 diffs against the old commit `ae4ad52` and still fails after the commit; every flagged line is the oven film in `OvenStep.tsx` (2026-11-03), none from the Buy-all change, so the test is stale.
