## Dessert Run (was Plating Belt), the eighth mini-game: PREVIEW ONLY (developer 2026-11-16; committed d3e3fe2, art/desserts redo UNCOMMITTED)

- **Why it was redone:** the developer played the Plating Belt (2026-11-16): "not even understanding how this game works", the art did not fit, the mosaic did not look like a dish. They asked for the mechanics of Ants Foraging: Color Dash, with waiters taking pieces of a dessert to guests. Decisions: the waiters RUN, a fixed dessert GALLERY by tier (never the menu's dish of the day), the name **Dessert Run**. The 2026-11-15 Plating Belt plan and its art prompts are superseded; the old belt, rim, tables, undo, lap and dish-of-the-day code and its QA are deleted.
- **Status:** rules, four desserts, 420 proven plates, a screen with the painted art, QA and a browser test, all passing. NOT in the daily routine, no state, money, ad or analytics yet. Open `/?dessertRun=18` (also 28, 45, 65; `?platingBelt` no longer exists). **The gate is still open: the developer must play it on a real phone and say it is fun** before the routine slot, rewards or ads (production rules). Option A (decided 2026-11-15, still the plan): from Level 18 the Car Park and Dessert Run alternate business days.
- **How it plays:** a hand-made dessert is a 28 x 28 mosaic of cubes on a plate (mosaic pictures built like the developer's Colony Flow lily-pad reference, redone three times on 2026-11-16: the WHOLE plate disc is filled (500 cubes each): a two-tone background (light field, darker under a wavy line) with white splash bars, the dessert on it with a navy outline one cube thick, a one-cube cream edge on the lit side and a darker edge on the other, 8 to 9 vivid colours, clusters only (sprinkles in pairs, no lone cube). The background and the outline are layers to peel before the dessert inside is reachable. Cookie on cyan and blue water, donut on mint, cherry cupcake on butter yellow, strawberry ice-cream cone on lilac. Earlier tries: procedural blobs ("not a real dish"), fat glossy button cubes with noisy tones ("still bad"), a flat dessert alone on a bare plate ("not arranged close, colours bad")). Five standby spots; 3 to 5 lanes of numbered waiters where only the front one is tappable. A tap sends it to a free spot; there it fetches the cube of its colour nearest the aisle that touches the OUTSIDE (the grid edge, or an empty cell joined to it; a donut's hole counts only once opened), one at a time; a runner takes each to the serving cart and on to one of four guests (by quarters of the plate). At 0 the waiter bows and leaves. A waiter with nothing reachable waits and goes on by itself; all five waiting = jam; "Try again" is the same plate. No clock, no undo. Speed x1 / x2 / x4 (the fixed step runs more often); holding a waiter lights the cubes it can reach. Stars by the most waiters blocked at once (at most 2 = 3 stars, 3 or 4 = 2, 5 = 1).
- **Every shipped plate is proved by replay, not by the builder alone:** the deck is built by taking the dessert apart in a sequence that can always continue, then dealt into lanes in a shuffled pattern that keeps each lane in deck order; sending waiters in deck order should win. With the 7 to 8 colour pictures about 5 to 10 % of candidate seeds did NOT win on replay (the real rules pick the nearest reachable cube at run time, which can differ from the build), so `scripts/make-dessert-seeds.mts` keeps only the seeds that win, on time and with taps up to 4 s late, and `dessert-run-qa` replays all 420 shipped plates again. The lanes' fronts can be far ahead of the deck order, which is what makes a jam possible. Careless play (a random front waiter whenever a spot is free) wins 80 / 75 / 57 / 30 % by tier (QA, 2026-11-17; `narrow` 0.5 / 0.75 / 0.9 / 1, tier 3 and 4 waiters of 4 and 3 to 9 cubes; tier 4 serves the donut and the cupcake because the cone is mostly open background).
- **Files:** `business/dessertRun.ts` (the whole game, pure: stage geometry, `makeDeck`, `makeRun`, `tapLane`, `stepRun`, `isJammed`, `playDeckOrder`, `runDeckProblem`), `dessertGallery.ts` (the pixel-art desserts, a small region painter that works out the outline and the light and dark edges, and 35 tones in 5 to 9 colour GROUPS per dessert: a waiter fetches a whole group, the picture draws each cube in one of its group's tones (`Dessert.tones()`, `cellHex`), so a picture can have smooth shading while the game keeps 5 to 6 waiter colours; `GRID` 28, `CELL` 13.5, plate radius 218), `dessertRunContent.ts` (`dessertRunFor(level, day, round)`), `dessertSeeds.ts` (generated by `scripts/make-dessert-seeds.mts`, then prettier; regenerate after ANY change to a dessert or to the deck builder), `components/kc/inventory/DessertRunGame.tsx` (screen; the game loop runs the fixed step on a canvas), `dessertRunDraw.ts` (drawing; each mosaic tile is blitted 1:1 at its exact device-pixel size (`tileSprite`) and fills its whole box: no gap, no rounded corner, no scaling; its edge is the developer's reference strip of 2026-11-17: a bright top edge, a fade from light to deeper, a shaded bottom and right edge, a small glint (a flat tile with no edge was tried first and the picture lost its definition); painted pieces with a plain fallback each; guests sit on the chairs: table, then guest, then the table's front over them), `dessertRunArt.ts` (loader and the magenta-to-colour waiter recolour). Test hook `?dessertRun=<level>` (`App.tsx`, `ScreensRouter.tsx`, screen id `dessert-run`).
- **Art (Codex, 2026-11-16):** `Dessert Run Assets/*.png` (local, gitignored) built by `py scripts/make-dessert-run-assets.py` into `public/business/dessertrun/*.webp` (16 files, 760 KB: backdrop, plate, aisle, table, guest plate, cart, spots, counter, guests x 3 moods, two waiters x stand/run sheets, win, jam, sparkle). The waiters are magenta-keyed and recoloured to the cube colours in the browser once. `DR-puff` is built by Codex but not used yet. Prompts: `docs/planning/DESSERT-RUN-CODEX-PROMPTS-2026-11-16.md`. Plan and demo: `docs/planning/DESSERT-RUN-PLAN-2026-11-16.md`.
- **Tests:** `scripts/dessert-run-qa.mts` (A tiers, B gallery, C no randomness, D rules, E every plate, F difficulty band, G choice, H layout; about two minutes, most of it the Vite start), e2e `tools/e2e/dessertrun.mjs` (D1 to D8, all pass), `fullscreen.mjs` F5 changed to measure the new screen's root (the old check looked for the Plating Belt's brown panel; said here), `ui-copy-qa` and `lazy-load-qa` pass.
- **Not verified:** the feel on a real phone (tap size, speed, whether a jam is fair), the difficulty by a human, the recolour of the light colours (yellow and pink) on a real screen, the four pictures at phone size (checked in screenshots only), the gallery beyond four desserts (the plan wants about twelve), the routine slot, rewards, ads, analytics, store text and screenshots (`belt_slot` in `public/playgama-bridge-config.json` is NOT added; flag it when it is).

## Kitchen Dispatch REBUILT as a drag-to-the-gate puzzle, the ninth mini-game: PREVIEW ONLY (developer 2026-11-17)

The first version ("tap a crate and it slides along its own arrow") was the Car Park again and played badly ("very bad and not even good to play"). The developer sent a screenshot of a sliding-brick game and asked to copy its mechanism. Researched (the Color Block Jam family; the official FAQ and store page confirm: slide blocks freely, each door takes blocks of ONE colour, clear every block before the timer ends, blockers, power-ups; they do NOT state the exact drag rule, the gate width rule, the shapes or the power-up effects, so those come from the screenshot and from Claude's reading, listed below).

- **The game now:** a counter with ingredient CRATES of different sizes (1x2, 2x2, 1x3, 2x3 ...), each in its ingredient's colour with a stud on every cell and the ingredient on the stud, plus dark FIXED blocks. The player DRAGS a crate in any direction (it follows the finger cell by cell, stops against another crate or a dark block, slides along an obstacle). Every ingredient has a GATE of its colour in the wooden frame; push a crate against its own gate, its width inside the gate, and it leaves. Clear every crate before the clock runs out (the only way to lose; nothing can jam). Boosters: Freeze (the clock stops 10 s, 2), Rocket (tap a crate: it goes out, 2), Hammer (tap a dark block: smashed, 2), Undo (takes back the last drag or the last crate out, 3). Stars by time left (45 % = 3, 20 % = 2). Clock 40 s + 9 s a crate. Open `/?kitchenDispatch=18` (also 28, 45, 65) or the Kitchen's Mini-games sheet (the player's own level): 5x5 / 6x6 / 7x7 / 7x7, 5-6 / 8-9 / 9-10 / 11-12 crates, 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 ingredients, gates 2 / 3 / 3 / 3 cells wide. **Not in the daily routine; no save state, money, rewards, ads or analytics. The gate is open: the developer plays it on a real phone first.**
- **Built so every board can be won:** `crateBoardFor` builds backwards: crates are placed last-to-leave first, and each new crate is kept only if it can reach its own gate past the ones already there (`exitSteps`, a breadth-first search over where the crate can slide). Taking them in the reverse order always works, and `solveBoard` (everyone else standing still) finds it. Difficulty is measured in "waves" (the crates that can go now, then the ones they were holding up ...): the tier needs at least 3 / 4 / 5 / 6 waves and at most 4 / 3 / 3 / 2 crates free at the start. The crate sizes are weighted toward the small ones (a board of big crates cannot be placed). Gates are placed from shuffled legal slots, a dark block never sits in a gate's mouth. Every move can be taken back, and a crate that leaves only makes room, so no move spoils a board (proved by taking 40 random drags back, `crate-dispatch-qa` C2; the strict "others stand still" check is not used on scrambled boards, it is stricter than play).
- **Assumptions (not in the sources, change on the developer's word):** an exit needs the crate flush against the gate with its whole width inside the gate (the screenshot's gates look 1-2 cells wide); a crate leaves the moment it touches its gate (no extra push); Rocket sends one crate out wherever it is; Hammer smashes a dark block; the star rule; the clock and the booster counts; no lives. The sliding-brick games sell boosters for coins and lives; none of that exists here.
- **Files:** `business/crateDispatch.ts` (pure: `crateBoardFor`, `solveBoard`, `exitSteps`, `gateTouched`, `brickFits`, `startRound`, `dragBrick`, `finishDrag`, `undoRound`, `freezeRound`, `rocketBrick`, `hammerWall`, `crateStars`, the four tiers; it no longer uses the Car Park), `components/kc/inventory/KitchenDispatchGame.tsx` (screen: a canvas for the room, the board and the crates; real HTML for the HUD, the clock and the four boosters; pointer down / move / up with capture, an armed Rocket or Hammer waits for a tap, crates glide toward their cell), `kitchenDispatchDraw.ts` (bricks, dark blocks, gates and the exit animation are DRAWN IN CODE), `kitchenDispatchArt.ts` (loads only the room, tile, frame, puff, counter and the 8 ingredient pictures). The painted `CD-crate-*` and `CD-gate` pictures are no longer used (still built by `scripts/make-crate-dispatch-assets.py`). The Car Park's `generateLotFor` split stays (harmless). Screen id `kitchen-dispatch`, hook `?kitchenDispatch=<level>`.
- **Art:** `Crate Dispatch/CD-*.png` built into `public/business/cratedispatch/*.webp`. Plain art now: Rocket and Hammer are emoji (🚀 🔨) on the painted round button; Freeze and Undo use the painted icons. Codex prompts for painted bricks (studs, bevel), a dark block, a gate, a rocket and a hammer icon can be written if wanted.
- **Tests:** `scripts/crate-dispatch-qa.mts` (A: 150 boards in each tier winnable, well formed, as hard as the tier says, deterministic, harder tier = more waves; B the drag; C every board cleared by the REAL `dragBrick` cell by cell and 40 random drags taken back; D Undo; E boosters, clock, stars; F source), e2e `tools/e2e/kitchendispatch.mjs` (K1-K11 with real mouse drags: a drag moves a crate and a dark block stops it, Freeze, Undo, Rocket, Hammer, a full clear by dragging, the clock running out, Try again, 48 px buttons at five phone sizes). `lazy-load-qa` L1 counts thirteen lazy Restaurant screens (unchanged by the rebuild).
- **Second look (developer 2026-11-17, from his mockup "Level 55"; UNCOMMITTED):** the screen now follows the mockup. Crates are painted wooden boxes with the ingredient big on every cell (no studs), the dark blocks are riveted grey metal plates, the empty cells are warm dark wood with a faint chef's hat, the gates are thicker plates (36 stage units) sitting on the board's edge with a white ring round the ingredient, the four boosters are the mockup's blue squircle buttons (painted Freeze with a snowflake, Rocket, Hammer, Undo; no more emoji) with the count badge at the corner, the level badge reads "Level N", Paige (her own `cutout-explain`) stands beside the instruction scroll, a pinned tomato card carries the tip, the crates pill has a small crate icon, the three stars are bigger, and the short notices ("Smashed!" and so on) replace the instruction text instead of sitting over the board. `scripts/make-kd-ui-assets.py` cuts the four buttons out of `Crate Dispatch/CD-reference-mockup-55.png` (gitignored, like the other CD-* pictures) and the mascot out of Paige's cutout (`booster-*.webp`, `mascot.webp`). The floor tile picture is no longer loaded. Tests unchanged and passing (`crate-dispatch-qa`, e2e `kitchendispatch` K1-K11, `minigames`). Not matched on purpose: the mockup's girl (a different character; Paige is the game's own) and the mockup's wooden frame (the existing painted frame is kept).
- **Not verified:** the feel on a real phone (the drag, the glide, the pace of the clock, finger size against a 7x7 counter), the difficulty by a human (waves are a measure, not a person), 7x7 crates under a thumb, Level 91 tuning, a painted look for the new pieces.

## Car Park, the seventh mini-game and the last step before START (developer 2026-11-15; committed d87232d, backdrop fix UNCOMMITTED)

- **What it is:** guests' cars and the neighbour's share one lot with an open street on ALL FOUR sides (the first backdrop had a building down each side and a sideways car drove into the wall, found on a real phone 2026-11-15; the new `car-park-backdrop-v2.png` has our cafe and the neighbour's building only as small corner pieces, the lot is 86 % of the stage width and vehicles drive off the stage edge they face). Tap a vehicle and it drives out along its arrow if nothing is in its way; a blocked one HONKS and costs a patience heart. Three hearts, three strikes: the third honk is "the neighbour called a tow truck", and "Try again" is the SAME lot, one tap. Stars: 0 honks 3, 1 honk 2, 2 honks 1; a **Tow** (one free vehicle removed, even a blocked one) costs a star; one more tow can be had once a business day with a rewarded ad (`car_park_tow`, never needed, no money so no ledger entry). The hint after 4 s idle glows a vehicle that can leave. Pause has Resume / Restart this lot / Leave.
- **Where:** the LAST step of the opening routine (`FlowStepId` "carpark", step 6, "Clear the car park"), once a business day, from **Level 15** (after every other mini-game has been met). The Prep Cook clears it with no stars only from Level 30 (`canPrepCookClearCarPark`); the "skip this step" ad works as for the others. Rewards: Hype by the usual star table and Paige's praise (`PraiseGame` "car-park"); no money, no stock, no ledger entry.
- **Rules (pure, `game/business/carPark.ts`):** `generateLot(level, seed)` drops vehicles in one at a time and keeps each only if the lot can still be emptied (the "blocked by" graph stays acyclic: solvable BY CONSTRUCTION), then flips driving directions to the tier's difficulty (few vehicles free at the start, a long chain) while it stays solvable; same level and seed is the same lot. `CAR_PARK_TIERS` (Level 15 / 22 / 32 / 45 / 65: 5x5 to 6x9 cells, 5 to 18 vehicles, cars of 2 cells, vans 3, buses 4). `lotFor(save, level)` seeds from level, business day and rounds played. State `business.carPark` `{ rounds, stars, best, perfect, streak, howto, doneDay, towAdDay }` (optional, no migration).
- **Screen:** `components/kc/inventory/CarParkGame.tsx`. The lot is a grid of SQUARE cells sized from the stage (78 % of its width is the lot, the rest the two buildings), vehicles are drawn once pointing up and turned by CSS, a vehicle that leaves drives off the lot (`carparkDrive`), a blocked one shakes and shows the honk burst. Test hook: `?carPark=<level>` (as the other mini-games). 320 x 568: a 6-column vehicle is 41 px wide (50 px at 390), a little under the usual 48 px; if a real phone says it is too small the late lots can shrink the side buildings.
- **Art (Codex, 2026-11-15):** `Car Park Assets/*.png` (local, gitignored) built by `py scripts/make-car-park-assets.py` into `public/business/carpark/*.webp` (backdrop 720 x 1280, 8 cars, 8 vans, 4 buses, tow truck, neighbour faces, hearts, honk, puff); a missing picture shows a plain box. The buses exist in blue / yellow / green / white only, so the generator never gives a bus another colour (the QA checks every vehicle has its picture). The tow truck, the puff and the neighbour's "angry" face beyond the failed card are built but not all used yet.
- **Tests:** `scripts/car-park-qa.mts` (A: 500 seeds in each of the 5 tiers solvable, in bounds, vehicle count, difficulty, deterministic, a neighbour and a guest in every lot, every picture exists, builds in under 800 ms; B moves and tow; C stars; D routine, once a day, Prep Cook, no money), e2e `tools/e2e/carpark.mjs` (P1-P9: all pass). Updated to the new rule: `audio-qa` S1 (sixteen effects: the Car Park horn) and `mini-game-praise-qa` A1 / W2 (six mini-games).
- **Not verified:** the feel on a real phone (tap size at 320, the drive-off, the honk), the difficulty of the five tiers by a human, the rewarded-ad path (there is no ad in the test build), the tow truck art in play.

## Paige praises a perfect mini-game round (developer 2026-11-14, UNCOMMITTED)

- Finishing a REAL round of Fridge Sort, Stock the shelves, Delivery Inspection, Sauce Station or the Fridge Check with 3 stars makes Paige cheer or clap in her big bubble (green leaf, "Got it") the next time the player is on the **kitchen home page** (not the map, where the Fridge Check opens). Rules: `restaurant/miniGamePraise.ts` (pure: 4 lines per game, a second perfect round in a row says "another three stars", three or more "on fire"; the line rotates with how many she has said, no clock or random); App `notePraise` (called from the four finish handlers and `cleanlinessAction` for the check).
- Only a hired Paige with tips ON says it; a practice round earns none (it never reaches the app); a round under 3 stars ends the run. It waits behind the "Next step" guide card and any other calm blocker, is said once, and is NEVER written to her saved tips or the notebook (the id starts `praise:`). No money, no save field. Analytics `assistant_praise_shown { game, level }`.
- Tests: `scripts/mini-game-praise-qa.mts` (new, A1-A6, B1-B6, W1-W5), e2e `fridgecheck` T20 (Paige hired, tips on: 3-star check, back on the kitchen, bubble `praise:fridge-check:0`, tone success, not in the saved tips). The other four mini-games use the same call; only the Fridge Check is driven in the browser. Not verified: a real phone; the wording (the developer may want more or different lines: one table, `PRAISE_LINES`).
- Harness note: `shot()` writes screenshots to a stray `Knife%20Craft/tools/e2e/out` folder beside the repo (the space in the path is URL-encoded by `OUT`); not fixed.

## Restart in the pause card of the five mini-games (developer 2026-11-14, UNCOMMITTED)

- Fridge Sort, Stock the shelves, Delivery Inspection, Sauce Station and the Fridge Check had only Resume and Leave on the pause card. Each now has **↻ Restart** (`*-restart`): the SAME board / crate / sauce / night again from the top, in the Ready state (clock full; Start or the first touch starts it). Cutting and Prep Rush already had a restart; the cleaning game has no pause.
- A restart inside a round takes nothing: only the end of a round pays or counts, so no money, stars, Hype or ledger entry move, and the practice flag is kept. ("Play again" after a result is unchanged: same board, practice, no reward.)
- How: Fridge Sort `restart(asPractice)` ("Play again" = `restart(true)`), Stock the shelves `resetFor(round)`; the other three wrappers keep a `restarts` counter in the `Round` key (not `attempt`, so the same cards / items / sauce are dealt).
- Tests: e2e `fridgesort` S9r, `stockroomsort` T7r, `deliveryinspection` I9r, `saucestation` S2c, `fridgecheck` T5b (all pass). The other suites were not run (no rule changed).

## Prep Cook on the "Before you open" sheet, a better Sauce Station (developer 2026-11-13, three requests, UNCOMMITTED)

- **Prep Cook on the sheet** (`DeliveryRoutine.tsx` `ServiceGateSheet`, App `prepCookDoesStep`, `routineStepDone`): from Level 30 (`staffHireFrom("prep-cook")`) a player who has not hired him sees "Hire a Prep Cook · $X a day" (hiring is free, he is paid by the hour at closing: no money, no ledger entry); once hired, the button "Prep Cook does <next step>" does it right there (check the delivery, unpack, stock the shelves, the sauce; not the Fridge Check, the Cleaner's chore): no stars, Hype or pins, the same as his button inside each game (`PREP_COOK_STEPS`). The mini-games stay mandatory in the sense of the routine: the step is done, not removed. Analytics `routine_step_prep_cook`. e2e `deliveryflow.mjs` F6b-F6d.
- **Sauce whisk follows the finger** (`SauceParts.tsx` `ToolInBowl`): it is positioned on every animation frame from a ref (no React render per pointer move, no CSS easing; it trailed the finger before) and circles by itself while Auto whisks (it stood still). The angle maths in `SauceStationGame` is unchanged.

Any action clears it; Reduced Motion: no animation. (Tiers that hide the recipe get the whisk / Bottle hints only.)

Built from the same results as the stars, so it cannot disagree; `MixResult` gained `blended`, `split`, `thickSide`.

The tile height scales with the screen (`clamp(56px, 9vh, 84px)`); up to 10 items use 5 columns, a longer shelf uses as many 48 px columns as fit (7 at 390 px, so the bowl keeps about 205 px). **Known:** on a 320 x 568 phone with a long late shelf (13 items) the bowl area is small (3 rows); a smaller top card on tiny phones would give it back. Checked: `saucestation.mjs`, `saucevisual.mjs`, `sauce-station-qa`. Prompts: docs/planning/SAUCE-PRODUCTS-CODEX-PROMPTS-2026-11-13.md (delivered).

The card was always there from Level 2; nothing pointed to it.

## The bigger fridge before the kitchen is stuck, the health inspector's visit, the basket bonus (developer 2026-11-13, UNCOMMITTED)

- **The bigger fridge is asked for BEFORE the kitchen is stuck** (`restaurant/fridgeUpgradeNeed.ts`, card `FridgeNeedCard.tsx` on the Pre-Service Check). The check projects the shelf two services ahead (what is on it, plus today's purchases, minus what is used): "full" = today's food does not fit, "soon" = a service within two levels would not fit. The simulated player who never buys is asked at Level 44 and was stopped at 46.
  - The wallet covers it (for "soon": the fridge AND today's food): the purchase is **required**, START reads "Buy the fridge to start", one button buys it on the sheet.
  - Short by no more than 25 %: **"Watch · 25% off"** (new rewarded placement `fridge_discount`, once a day, only on `rewarded`, on the latest save). The ad pays the discount as one "rewarded-ad" entry and the fridge is bought at its full price in the same save, so the ledger and the wallet agree. Never needed: a wallet further away keeps the supplier's cover of 2026-11-12 (`fridgeStuckFor`).
  - Analytics `fridge_need_bought { fridge, level, reason, ad }`, `fridge_discount_ad { status }`. Tests: `fridge-need-qa` (new), `playables-ads-qa` E6, e2e `precheckvisits.mjs` F1-F5.
- **The health inspector visits during the campaign** (`restaurant/healthInspector.ts`, card `HealthInspectorCard.tsx`; plan: docs/planning/HEALTH-INSPECTOR-PLAN-2026-11-13.md). Until now an inspection only ran at Level 91. From Level 13 he is at the door on the Pre-Service Check: five live checks (cleaning tasks, expired food, fridge room, dish soap, cleaning liquid), each with a fix button, then "Let him in" (START waits for that one tap). Grade A: +20 / 30 / 40 Hype in a row, back in 6 levels; B: nothing, back in 4; C: a fine of 5 % of the level's pay (one "inspection-fine" entry, nothing when the wallet cannot pay), back in 2. State `business.healthInspector` (optional, no migration). Analytics `health_inspection`. Tests: `health-inspector-qa` (new), e2e `precheckvisits.mjs` I1-I4.
- **Basket cap stays 3; the big-delivery bonus is +10 Hype a basket** (was +5), so three baskets earn the full +20 again (`BIG_DELIVERY_HYPE_PER_BASKET`; the Stock the shelves boards share the rule). Tests changed to the rule: `fridge-sort-qa` M1 / M4, `stockroom-sort-qa` F7.
- **Simulator:** `restaurantCampaignSim` buys a required fridge and lets the inspector in, as the game makes the player do (`stats.fridgeNeedAt`, `inspections`, `inspectionFines`). The completionist ends with about $300,400 (was $301,700: the fines of a player who never cleans before the Cleaner is hired); the frozen campaign baseline is untouched.
- **Browser tests:** seeded saves keep the inspector away by default (`INSPECTOR_AWAY` in `tools/e2e/harness.mjs`), as they keep Paige quiet.
- **Not built / yours to decide:** an ad for a second look after a bad grade, money for a Grade A, a painted inspector, a warning one level before (plan section 7). Delivery Inspection's smallest items stay as they are (your "okay").

## Deep check 2: what the bigger purchases broke (developer 2026-11-12, "find the mistakes of the game, check deeply")

Swept the prep batch with throw-away scripts over every level (a Temporary Shortage day at Levels 3-91, baskets per shopping trip at Levels 4-91, a whole campaign with a player who never buys a fridge) and read every caller. Five mistakes, all from the morning's change, all fixed:

- **A Temporary Shortage day (one day in nine) broke the Market's own buttons.** One purchase is at most 10 units, and since the batch a plan row is often 11-70 units: on such a day 73 of the 89 stock levels had rows that "Buy all" silently skipped and that a row's button answered "not available today"; the standing-order delivery skipped them too. Now a row over the limit is bought as several ordinary purchases (`restaurant/purchaseParts.ts`: all or nothing, one ledger entry each) by "Buy all", a row's button (it says "2 deliveries") and the morning delivery. The prices on the buttons and the check's "Missing ... $" are the sum of those purchases (`partsCost`), so a tap charges what it shows. The supplier's own rule is untouched (the ingredient's card still sells 10 at a time and says so). `prep-batch-qa` H1-H5, H7; `restaurant-deliveries-qa` S1; browser `shortagebuy.mjs` B1-B6.
- **A two-day "Buy all" became up to 15 baskets**, each to be checked AND unpacked before the restaurant may open (about half an hour at Level 50; 5-7 baskets already at Levels 11-13). One waiting delivery is now at most **3 baskets** (`MAX_BASKETS_WAITING` in `business/fridgeSort.ts`), each full size; the biggest ingredients lose a piece first so every ingredient stays in the game; the Market's basket hint is capped the same way. The "+20 Hype for five baskets" bonus tops out at +10 (three baskets) now. `fridge-sort-qa` M2, M2b (new), M3, M4 changed to the rule.
- **A walk-in's reserve was too small.** A walk-in must leave the next level's own orders alone, but that reserve was batched at the walk-in's level, not the next one (smaller wherever the batch or the row cap grows, such as Level 22 to 23), so a walk-in could eat into the next service's stock. The reserved orders carry their own level now (`prepBatch.atLevel`, read by `orderRequirements`). `prep-batch-qa` H6.
- **The Basic fridge can be too small with no way out.** A simulated player who never upgrades is stopped at Level 46 ("Fridge full"); with the money for the food but not for the $2,000 fridge, nothing was offered (CLAUDE section 7: no soft-lock). In exactly that case the Pre-Service Check now offers the usual cover under "Fridge full" (an ad, or supplier credit): `serviceCover.fridgeStuckFor`, the plan's `fridgeStuck`. A player who can pay for the fridge is still sent to it. `restaurant-service-cover-qa` X1-X4.
- **A split purchase filled the basket with its last part only** (the crate read one amount per ingredient): the amounts are summed now (`App.persistIngredientPurchases`).

Checked and left as they are: guests' and walk-ins' recipes keep their "one plate" mark through the whole service (the object is never copied); no screen states an old mini-game number; the day-end inspection that fines a full fridge runs only at Level 91 (Professional fridge); a rewarded skip or the Prep Cook clears one basket, so three skips a day now cover half of the biggest delivery.

To decide (yours): Delivery Inspection's smallest items are about 40 px wide on a 320 px phone at the last tier (the magnifier and drag-aside are there); `MAX_BASKETS_WAITING` (3) and the Hype bonus are one constant each; the simulator has a `noFridgeUpgrade` option for this study. Stale browser tests seen again, not changed: `marketneeds.mjs` step 3 (the Daily Check shortcut), `restaurantcover`, `restaurantmeasures`.

## Tougher mini-games and a restaurant that buys a lot more (developer 2026-11-12, player reviews, pushed a932aad)

Plan and numbers: docs/planning/MINIGAME-TOUGHNESS-AND-PURCHASES-PLAN-2026-11-12.md. Suite: `prep-batch-qa` (new).

- **The prep batch** (`restaurant/prepBatch.ts`, pure): from Level 3 a level's OWN orders use `prepBatchAt(level)` times their plate portion (3x at Level 3, 4x at 8, 5x at 14, 6x at 23, 7x at 40, 8x at 70, smooth; one
  ingredient of one order at most 4 units until Level 22, 6 after). ONE function, `campaignStock.orderRequirements(save, recipe, which, level)`, feeds the Pre-Service Check, the serve, the Market plan and the level goals, so the check
  and the serve agree and nothing is left over. **Menu guests and walk-ins stay one plate** (`asPlate`, a WeakSet marker on a copy in `menuGuests.ts` / `orderPool.ts`). No pay, reward, ledger rule or save field moved; the
  frozen campaign baseline does not read it (`prep-batch-qa` D5).
- **Result** (own orders, empty fridge): Levels 4-20 deliver about 20 pieces a service (about 8 before), the whole campaign's food bill is about $3,300 (was $610, against about $299k earned), one service needs at most 17 fridge units
  until Level 22 and 38 after (Basic 40, Commercial 100). The completionist still ends above $150k; the campaign simulation, Endless and the fridge-pressure suites pass.
- **The games ask for more:** Unpack the delivery baskets 12 / 15 / 18 / 20 / 22 pieces (were 8-16; one ingredient up to 6-12 pieces, `piecesCapAt`), shelves 4 / 6 / 7 / 9 / 10, tighter clocks (x1.5 / 1.25 / 1.12 / 1.08 / 1.05); Delivery
  Inspection 12 / 14 / 17 / 20 / 24 items with 3 / 4 / 4-5 / 5-6 / 6-8 bad ones and longer clocks (70-55 s); Stock the shelves boards 3 / 5 / 7 / 9 / 9 sets and a purchase makes up to 4 sets (1, 2-3, 4-6, 7+ packs);
  Fridge Check 8-16 cards at least with 2-5 Bin and 2-4 Use next; Sauce Station 65 / 62 / 58 / 54 s clocks and a tighter colour tolerance (x1.3 / x1.1).
- **Stars stay fair:** `fridge-sort-qa` H4b walks every level and basket size 4-22: one wrong drop never earns three stars. STAR_AT unchanged; the late tiers' clock factors went up a little so an average player still gets a star 95% of the time.
- **Temporary Shortage** (one day in nine, one purchase at most 10 units): a late row can need 18-22 units (chicken Level 55, steak Level 80), so the player buys 10 and the rest in a second tap; the simulated player now does that (`restaurantCampaignSim.mts`).
  The event's own number is untouched. The Basic fridge holds a whole day's plan only until about Level 23 (the completionist buys the Commercial fridge there, the Professional at 26).
- **Tests changed to the new rule (CLAUDE section 8):** `fridge-sort-qa`, `stockroom-sort-qa`, `fridge-check-qa`, `difficulty-ramp-qa`, `restaurant-stock-qa`, `walkin-qa`, `restaurant-market-needs-qa`, `iphone-fixes-qa`, `restaurant-economy-pass-qa`, `oven-step-qa` (E2 allowlist),
  `restaurantCampaignSim.mts`, browser `fridgesort.mjs` / `deliveryinspection.mjs` / `fridgecheck.mjs`. Looked at in Chrome at 390 x 844 and 320 x 568 (a 21-piece basket, a 24-item crate).
- **Not done / to look at:** the biggest basket and crate on a real phone; if the live `level_completed` shows a cliff at Levels 3-14, lower `PREP_BATCH_TIERS` there first (one table). The restaurant's supplies (tableware, napkins) follow
  covers and tables, not the batch: the Stock the shelves boards grew through their tier numbers and the sets per purchase only.

## Deep check for logical mistakes (developer 2026-11-12)

Swept the new rules with throw-away scripts over every level and many seeds (inspection crates Levels 3-100 x 25 seeds, Stockroom boards 6-100, Fridge Sort baskets 4-100, Fridge Check decks and wrap shelves 10-100 x 4 stocks x 3 salts, the delivery routine's no-soft-lock walk Levels 3-60 x 8 states), then fixed what they and a read-through found:

- **Fridge Check deck was all Bin** with a fridge full of spoiled food (no Keep, no Use next): now always a mix (`checkCards`: the tier's minimum Bin and Use next first, the other real ones next, a quarter of the deck left for Keep). `fridge-check-qa` A13.
- **How-to said "Every card is real food in your fridge"**, no longer true (training cards): now "Cards show your fridge food. Quiet nights add a few extra."
- **Paige's new art files** were requested again (a 404) every time she gave a tip: a missing cutout / nameplate is remembered per pose for the visit (any subset of the twelve can be delivered).
- **The Cleaner safety net in `App.tsx`** now works from the LATEST save (`saveRef`), so two changes in one tick cannot be overwritten by an older copy.
- Clean results (no change needed): all inspection crates fair at every level (the smooth ramp included), every stockroom board solvable and within 10 cubbies, every Fridge Sort round packed within its limit, no routine step that its Prep Cook / ad skip cannot clear.
- **Four old failing suites fixed** (rules had changed, tests were stale): `journey-qa` J11 (the disposition doc regenerated by its own script), `oven-step-qa` E2 (the allowlist gained the two new pure files, with the dated comment), `phase11-qa` D1-D6 (cleaning starts at Level 4, chores are staged by cause: the restroom at 16, the dining reset later), `service-flow-qa` S4 (dine-in opens at Level 12, so Level 12 guests can be hurried: 70 % patience). Nothing was weakened: each assertion now states the current rule.
- Still stale: `tools/e2e/guide.mjs` G1/G3/G4b (seeds Level 6 with $20; the Second Floor opens at Level 11).

## Sauce Station polish (developer 2026-11-11, pushed)

- **Liquid in the pot:** the sauce is now a real SURFACE: the vessel's opening ellipse (`OPENING` in `SauceParts.tsx`, measured on the masks) moved down by how deep the sauce is, seen through the opening (`surfaceOf`), with a meniscus, a darker edge at the wall, a gloss and the swirl / slick / chunks inside it. It was a flat slab cut by the mask. The pot sits LOW in its space (`items-end pb-4`).
- **Play again** makes the SAME sauce (`sameSauceId`); "Next round" returns to the day's sauce.
- **Pour button** (`PourButton` in `SauceStationGame.tsx`): brass rim, glossy top, the sauce's colour rising inside as the bottle fills, "LET GO NOW!" with a glow and a 14 ms buzz on the line, "Too much!" past it, breathes when idle; Reduced Motion turns the animation off. `sauce-station-qa` M1-M5.

## Paige audit: every line checked against the game, Second Floor reminders (developer 2026-11-11)

Every Paige line (the 40 beats in `restaurant/assistant.ts`, the four chests, the Congratulations cards, the guide card in her voice) was read against the real rules (a script walked Levels 1-92 and listed what fires when). Found and fixed:

- **"Service N done! You're becoming a real chef"** fired one level EARLY (on the level about to be played, so "Service 10 done" with 9 done) and "real chef" is wrong at Service 80. Now `milestone:30..80` fire only once N services are DONE (`completed`), say "N services done!" with a true second half; 10 and 20 are left to their chests.
- **The Level 15 chest** said "Halfway to the finale! ... You're a real chef now!" (L15 is not halfway to anything): now "Fifteen services done! Your first steak is served!". The Level 20 chest "Chapter finale" is "Twenty services done!".
- **The finale tip** ("You did it!") fired on Level 90, two levels before the end: now only when the LAST level is done (91 services).
- **Level 27 staff tip** said "You can hire a team now" (the Server and Cashier are hired from Level 1-2): now "Services are getting busy. A Prep Cook will soon be needed" (the Prep Cook is required from Level 30). **Level 31 cuisine tip** now names the Indian Chef (the French Bistro at 23 needs none).
- **Fridge Check tip** said "practise it in Inventory": it is a mandatory closing chore with Bin / Use next. New **`sauce-daily`** tip at Level 10 (the Sauce Station is a daily step from then).
- **Second Floor reminders** (`restaurant/floorAdvice.ts`, `EARLY_TWO_MACHINES_BY_LEVEL` = 20): Paige reminds with the beats `floor-nudge:<kind>:<level>` (one per level and kind, repeatable), only when the player can really pay AND keep $300: `first-machine` (from Level 14), `second-machine` (one machine, before Level 20, from Level 12; the two cheap ones are Coffee $500 and Drinks $1,000), `new-machine` (the wallet covers the next machine + a polish step + the reserve) and `polish` (three polish steps + the reserve). Never two levels in a row; every second level before Level 20 while fewer than two machines, else every third. Not in the notebook; when her list is full the old reminders go first. The Kitchen guide card also says "Add a second machine" (`floor-machine2`). It nudges only: nothing is bought for the player, no money moves.
- Tests: `assistant-qa` C8/C9 (rule changed) and new I1-I13. No new art is needed for any of this (the poses exist).
- `tools/e2e/guide.mjs` G1/G3/G4b are stale (seed Level 6 with $20; the Second Floor opens at Level 11): not run green, not changed.

Delivery Inspection explains itself (tap the bad ones, drag fresh ones aside to look behind them, the magnifier) on the ready card, in the guide and in the in-game line; the 🔎 magnifier is in EVERY tier (30 / 22 / 16 / 12 / 10 s a round) because some phones cannot zoom.

A Pre-Service Check news line for the inspection. **Rewarded skip:** the "Before you open" sheet and the Inventory's routine card offer "Skip ... with an ad · N left today" for the next step (placement `skip_step`, 3 a day in `business.stepSkips`, only on `rewarded`, done as the Prep Cook does it: no stars/Hype, no money, no ledger; `App.skipRoutineStep`, `AdBonusApi.skipStep`).

NOT verified on a real iPhone (no device here): `audio-qa` X1-X3 only guard the wiring.
5. **"Buy everything needed" left cards behind:** the cards marked "needed soon" were never bought by the button. They now have their own button ("Also buy the N needed soon · $X", or "Buy all N now" when nothing is needed right now) in `MarketSupplies.tsx`.
6. **Cleaner hired but "Clean" still shown:** I could not reproduce it in the pure rules (the sim shows the Cleaner doing the boards at Level 4-12), so there is now a safety net: an effect in `App.tsx` runs `cleanerRound` whenever a Cleaner / Server / Prep Cook is on duty and a routine chore of theirs is open (idempotent, saves only when something was cleaned). If it still shows with a Cleaner, look at the Cleaner's AREAS chips (Kitchen off = the Cleaner skips the boards: `waitingReason` "area-off").
7. **More to check, gradually (plan doc):** `business/difficultyRamp.ts` moves every number smoothly between tiers (Fridge Sort basket + decoys, Stockroom Sort board, Delivery Inspection items + defects, Fridge Check cards); the Fridge Check always has Bin AND Use next cards (three piles from tier 1) and tops a quiet fridge up with seeded TRAINING cards (never real stock; `training: true`). `difficulty-ramp-qa`.
8. **Mandatory Sauce Station and Fridge Check:** step 5 of the delivery routine "Make today's sauce" from Level 10 (`SAUCE_REQUIRED_FROM`, `sauceDue`, `coverSauceToday`; the Prep Cook button and the ad skip meet the day's duty; state `sauceStation.doneDay`); the Fridge Check is always a game once freshness is live (no one-tap fallback; "Leave for now" only leaves the screen).

10. **Peeling: the OLD method is back** (the blade is under the finger at once; strip 0.34, done at 80%; `PreparationScene.tickPeel`, `PEEL` in `definitions.ts`). The curls of skin, the rustle and the buzz of the 2026-11-07 feel are kept. `tools/e2e/peelslow.mjs` P1/P2 updated to the new rule.

## Bugs and Issues, 10 items (developer 2026-11-11, UNCOMMITTED)

Source: "Bugs and Issues.docx" (OneDrive). One by one, each verified in a real browser (the Sauce Station in WebKit, the Safari engine, too).

1. **Second Floor purchases in plain words:** `restaurant/floorPurchaseText.ts` (pure: icon, what it does, "+$X an hour", the line after a buy with the real numbers); the goal strip reads "🪑 Coffee: add table #3 / +$25.95 an hour / $90.00 · ready" with an ⓘ; the station panel's three buttons are two lines ("Polish step 7 of 10 · $193.06 / Earns 6% more · +$5.09 an hour"); an ⓘ explains machine / table / polish / upgrade; after a buy: "✨ Coffee polished (7/10) · +$5.09 an hour · the floor earns $200.47 an hour". Tests: `floor-purchase-qa`, e2e `floorpurchase.mjs`.
2. **No ranking news at the first serve:** `rankChange` is silent before `RANKING_NEWS_FROM_LEVEL = 8` (config/earlyLevels.ts); that is both the Level Complete "#50 -> #49" row and Paige's Congratulations card; the guide's ranking beat uses the same constant. `restaurant-city-ranking-qa` C6, `firstrun.mjs` H6f.
3. **Peeling a little slow, more realistic:** `PEEL.MAX_SPEED` 3.4 (was 1.1: a whole potato about 6 s, was 10+) and `PEEL.FOLLOW_RATE` 16 (the blade closes the gap to the finger smoothly instead of crawling at a fixed pace; a flick is still held back). `peelslow.mjs` P2 now measures real time (2.5-9 s).
4. **Slice counts undone, speed kept:** `TAP_KNIFE.MIN_GAP_FRAC` 0.07 (a slice / chop is 12 cuts again, was 8) and julienne `requiredCuts` 10 (was 8). The tap-cut speed (320 ms), the knives' timing and Fever at x5 are untouched. `tap-cut-qa` B1/B2 and `julienne-fix-qa` 6 updated to the restored rule.
5. **The delivery routine, in order and mandatory:** `business/deliveryFlow.ts`: 1 CHECK the delivery (Delivery Inspection) -> 2 FRIDGE SORT (locked until the check is done) -> 3 STOCK THE SHELVES (supplies) -> 4 FRIDGE CHECK (the closing chores). While a step waits, "Open for service" does not start the service: the "Before you open" sheet (`business/DeliveryRoutine.tsx`) lists the steps in order with one button to the next; the same list is the TOP card of the Inventory (the games were below the fold). A basket that is unpacked takes its "checked" mark with it (`shiftInspected`); past today's five rewarded checks a check still counts (no reward); a hired Prep Cook has a button inside each game. The Fridge Sort chip is locked ("check it first") and a tap sends the player to the check. Level 1-2 and replays are never gated. Tests: `delivery-flow-qa`, e2e `deliveryflow.mjs` (+ `fridgesort.mjs` / `deliveryinspection.mjs` seeds).
6. **Screens appear with their pictures:** `common/ScreenReveal.tsx` wraps the router: a screen whose big pictures (backdrop, banner, map) are in view but not loaded stays hidden until they are loaded and decoded (cap 1.1 s; a lazy screen's late pictures get up to 12 s; a screen with its pictures ready is never held), and `game/preloadArt.ts` warms the Market / stockroom / map / second-floor pictures when the app is idle. e2e `screenreveal.mjs` (throttled network).
7. **Sauce Station "what to pour":** a step banner ("1 Pour: Olive oil ×3 · Lemon ×2 ..." / "2 Now circle the whisk in the green zone" / "3 Looks good! Tap Bottle it"), the recipe's tiles glow with "+3" and turn green with a tick when done, and the recipe chips count "0/3" up to a tick.
8. **Sauce bottling:** the pitcher turns about its spout tip, which sits over the bottle's mouth, and the stream falls from that tip to the sauce's surface (`PourScene`, `bottleMouth`); the sauce in a bottle is drawn on a canvas through the glass silhouette (`BottleLiquid`), so it can never be outside the glass on any browser (an iPhone showed it under the base); the bottles sheet clears 3 px at each cell border (a stray line from the neighbouring clasp).
9. **Whisk:** held handle UP and head down in the sauce (turned over, leaning toward the middle of the bowl by up to 24 degrees), the head is held inside the bowl's rim, it stands in the bowl at rest, a ripple under the head, and the circling is measured round the bowl's own middle and ellipse (`TOOL_BOWL`), so circling what you see is what counts. `sauce-station-qa` L1-L8, `saucevisual.mjs`.
10. **Dishwashing:** ROOT CAUSE: the sink's piece (key = its index) and its "Combo xN" chip (key = the streak) were siblings with number keys; at index 2 with a streak of 2 React saw the same key twice and DUPLICATED the piece (up to 23 stacked copies and scrub surfaces; the old pan stayed under the measuring cups, the rinse stuck at 95%). Now `piece-N` / `combo-N`; the Combo chip moved to the right (it covered the soap bottle). `dishwash-layout-qa` E1-E3 and e2e `dishwashpieces.mjs` (fails on the previous build, passes now).

- **Also:** an unrelated `git stash` rewrote four files with CRLF; they were put back to LF. `oven-step-qa` E2's allowlist gained `cityRanking.ts`, `restaurantGuide.ts` (display rules) beside the 11-10 files.
- **Not done / to look at on a phone:** the whisk tilt and the pour on a real iPhone (checked in WebKit on Windows, not on a device); the Fridge Check step has no Prep Cook button (a hired Cleaner clears the chore); the idle warming adds about 0.6 MB of background fetches once per visit.

## Sauce Station mini-game: BUILT with the art (developer request 2026-11-09, UNCOMMITTED)

- **What:** "Mix it, whisk it, bottle it". From **Level 7** (developer 2026-11-10: before Level 20; the Simple Garden Salad; sauces unlock 7 x3, 10, 12, 13 x2, 17, 18, 20, then 29+; plan docs/planning/FIRST-20-LEVELS-ENGAGEMENT-PLAN-2026-11-10.md; Paige beat `sauce-station`), **3 rounds a day** ("Sauce Station · 3/3" chip in Inventory above the fridge and in the Market's Ingredients tab; a hired Prep Cook has a "does it" button inside the game, no reward). STAGE 1: tap the 28-item pantry + the dish's produce to pour scoops (hold to repeat); match the target swatch and keep the thickness in the green band; blend by circling the whisk / spoon in the green speed zone (the dial; "Auto whisk" is the no-drag path); a dressing poured too fast into an unblended bowl SPLITS (blend back to 0, max 2 stars). STAGE 2: hold to pour the batch into 3-5 bottles and let go at the dashed fill line (a spill scores almost nothing; skipping the bottling caps at 2 stars). Stars, Hype (`HYPE_BY_STARS`), a Saucier pin every 3 perfect rounds (max 12) and the **Sauce Shelf** (screen `sauce-shelf`, 24 bottles, one per sauce mastered with 3 stars). **Never money, stock or the ledger.**
- **Rules:** `game/business/sauceColor.ts` (linear-light tint-weighted mix, CIE76 ΔE, match % = 100 - 2.2 ΔE, colour hints) and `game/business/sauceStation.ts` (PANTRY, the 24 SAUCES with reference recipes whose own mix IS the target, so each is solvable by construction; tiers from L23/40/55/75: cap 9-12 scoops, clock 75-60 s, help level, tolerance, oil limit, bottles 3-5 and fill window; blend/split/pour/bottle scoring; `finishRound`, `roundsLeft`, `sauceOfTheDay`). State `business.sauceStation = { mastered (bit per sauce), best, stars, perfect, rounds, streak, day, today, howto }` (optional, sanitised, no new top-level save field).
- **Screens:** `components/kc/sauce/` (`SauceStationGame.tsx`, `SauceShelf.tsx`, `SauceStationChip.tsx`, `SauceParts.tsx`, `sauceArt.ts`, generated `sauceSheets.ts`); lazy in `restaurantScreens.ts` (the Restaurant chunk now has ten lazy screens); ScreenIds `sauce-station`, `sauce-shelf`; `App.sauceStationAction` (analytics `sauce_station_started/finished/skipped/auto`, `sauce_mastered`). Back arrow before the clock starts, pause card, first-time how-to. Every painted piece has a CSS stand-in (the game plays with no art at all).
- **Art:** the 38 Codex files in `Sauce Station Assets/` (gitignored) are built by `python scripts/make-sauce-assets.py` into `public/business/sauce/` (843 KB lazy; unused pieces M10, M11, M19, M20, B07, B08 are not built; the vessels' front layers M03/M06 do not line up with M02/M05 so the liquid is clipped by the masks instead; C02 arrived 1536 x 1536 with the grid in the top 1024 rows and is cropped; the caps sheet carries a neck of sauce under every cap so only hand-picked cap boxes are shown) and writes `sauceSheets.ts` (the tight box of every cell).
- **Tests:** `sauce-station-qa` (colour maths, all 24 sauces solvable at every tier, thickness/blend/split/grade, bottling, finishing never touches money, wiring); e2e `tools/e2e/saucestation.mjs` (chip from L23, how-to, Back, pour/undo/cap, real pointer whisking, reference recipe + Auto = 100%, bottling by holding Pour, result, shelf, Prep Cook, 320 x 568, no art, no console errors). `lazy-load-qa` L1 updated to ten Restaurant screens (CLAUDE section 8: rule changed by the developer's new screens).
- **Open:** a real-phone pass (whisk circling by touch, tile size, bottle sizes); no new sound effects (the existing sort sounds are reused: pour/whisk/clink/cork/splash from the plan are not made); no rewarded-ad placement (needs the developer's yes); the Paige tip about it is not added; the shelf shows no recipe hints.

- **What:** a hidden-defect search over a crate of mixed produce: tap = flag NOT FRESH, drag = look behind, a 🔎 magnifier (tier 3+), a clock, a wrong flag costs 4 s. The plan's recommendations were taken for every decision (own chip beside "Unpack delivery", no real effect, 5 rounds a day, Prep Cook inspects for the player, name "Delivery Inspection"); the rewarded ad (D5) and the freshness top-up (D3) were NOT built (they need the developer's yes). Plan: `docs/planning/DELIVERY-INSPECTION-MINIGAME-PLAN-2026-11-08.md`; Codex prompts: `docs/planning/DELIVERY-INSPECTION-CODEX-PROMPTS-2026-11-08.md`.
- **Code:** `business/deliveryInspection.ts` (rules, seeded crate generator with fairness guarantees, tiers on Fridge Sort's levels 3 / 14 / 27 / 44 / 74, scoring, stars, state `business.deliveryInspection`), `inventory/DeliveryInspectionGame.tsx` + `inspection/InspectionParts.tsx` (the screen; painted art when present, CSS stand-ins when not), `DeliveryInspectionChip.tsx` (Market Ingredients tab + above the Inventory fridge), `ScreenId` "delivery-inspection", App `deliveryInspectionAction` (analytics `delivery_inspection_started / finished / skipped`), lazy in the Restaurant chunk. Rounds: one per basket of the waiting crate (`basketsLeft`), at most 5 a day; a Prep Cook has a button in the game. Reuses Fridge Sort's HUD, star bar, result panel and sounds (no new effects).
- **Art:** `Delivery Inspection Assets/` (local, gitignored: D01-D10 from the Codex kit) is built by `python scripts/make-inspection-assets.py` into `public/business/inspection/`: backdrop, crate-back, crate-front, defects (4 x 3), decoys (3 x 2), markers (2 x 2), magnifier delivered and used (181 KB); `pins.webp` (D10) is wired (PinArt) and shows when the file exists; D08 flies and D09 the guide clipboard and the optional T01/T02 spoiled twins are NOT built or used yet (add them to `JOBS` when used). The crate's geometry (`CRATE_ART_TOP`, `WALL_TOP`) matches the painted crate; nothing that matters is ever put behind its front wall.
- **Tests:** `delivery-inspection-qa` (opening, daily limit, Prep Cook, old saves, the crate's real ingredients, tiers, 400 crates per tier with every defect findable, scoring, stars, Hype, pins, money/stock/ledger untouched, wiring) and `tools/e2e/deliveryinspection.mjs` (chip, guide, start, wrong and right tap, drag, result, Done, Prep Cook, Skip, 320 px, tier 3 lens and decoys).
- **Fix 2026-11-08 (developer: "not seeing the new game, the pause button is stuck, no way back; rename the first game"):** (1) a hired Prep Cook used to HIDE the Inspect chip: now the chip always shows and the Prep Cook is a button inside the game ("🧑‍🍳 Prep Cook does it": the basket counts as inspected, no stars / Hype / pins / day count; `autoInspect`). (2) Before the first touch the clock is not running and the HUD button did nothing: in Fridge Sort, Stockroom Sort and Delivery Inspection it is now a ← Back arrow in the ready phase (leaves the game, the crate stays waiting) and the pause button once playing. (3) The first game is called **Fridge Sort** everywhere (chip "🧊 Fridge Sort · N", the how-to title, Pre-Service Check, Paige's line, the after-serve chip), was "Unpack delivery" / "Unpack the delivery". Tests updated to the new words (`early-levels-qa` C1-C3, `earlylevels.mjs`, `fridgesort.mjs` + S28 Back arrow, `deliveryinspection.mjs` I9 / I9a / I9c, `delivery-inspection-qa` A7).
- **Fix 2026-11-08 (developer: "ingredients too small, not properly in the basket, defects not blended"):** the ingredients are about 35% bigger and there are fewer (8-9 / 10 / 12 / 14 / 17 for the five tiers, was 8-10 / 12 / 15 / 18 / 22); the pile now sits IN the crate (the bottom row against the front wall, its base hidden by it, heaped above the rim) instead of floating above it; marks are cut to the ingredient's own outline (`BlendedMark`: the picture is a CSS mask, so nothing hangs off the edge) and the dull kinds are multiplied with the food's colours; marks are bigger (0.92 / 0.7 / 0.52 / 0.36 of the picture); a spoiled ingredient is also a little duller and browner all over, more for a big defect and not at all for a tiny one.
- **Deep check 2026-11-08 (bugs found and fixed):** (1) the nine-table stage's guest routes took ~0.7 s on a fast PC to build when the map opened (a phone would freeze for seconds): the walkable grid is cached per stage and reachability is a flood fill (now 12-47 ms). (2) The inspection timer's closure read an old render's found / wrong counts when the clock ran out: `end()` reads refs. (3) The transparent corners of a big ingredient took taps meant for the one under it (a wrong flag): the hit area is a circle. (4) Tiny defects were fully invisible outside the magnifier: now faint (0.3). (5) Ingredients can be flagged from the keyboard (Tab, Enter / Space). (6) The music scheduler could pile up bars after a long stall: it restarts at "now" if more than 0.5 s behind. Known stale, not ours: `tools/e2e/fridgecheck.mjs` T8 (tier numbers moved when the campaign became 91 levels, 60b4303); `fridgesort.mjs` S11 failed once when run beside other heavy jobs and passed twice alone.
- **Open:** a real-phone pass (tap vs drag precision, item counts per tier, clocks: the numbers are a first guess, the plan's §8 simulated-player tuning script is not written); D10 pins and the optional twins when the art arrives; the Paige / achievements hooks; the rewarded-ad placement and the freshness top-up if the developer says yes.

## Music: relaxing generated score, loading music (developer 2026-11-08, UNCOMMITTED)

- **Copyright-safe by construction:** no recorded, sampled, downloaded or copied sound anywhere (the game still ships no sound file). The score is composed at run time by `src/game/music/relaxComposer.ts` from plain scales (major, pentatonic), stacked-third chords on the scale's own degrees, common four-chord cycles and a seeded random walk for the melody; `AudioManager.ts` plays it with sine / triangle waves and filtered noise. Original to the game; nothing to license. `music-qa` C1-C6 guards it (no audio files, no `new Audio` / `decodeAudioData`, no music-site links, no unseeded randomness, no fetch).
- **For long sessions:** slow (52-64 beats a minute), no drums, a warm pad + a music-box melody + a soft low note + a "breath" of noise + now and then a high chime, through a generated 3 s reverb and a gentle limiter. It never repeats: a new four-chord cycle every 8 bars, the key moves every 16 bars (a fifth, a fourth, a second or a sixth), the melody has its own slow ~4 minute rise and fall, every 8th bar is a bar of rest, the first two bars are the pad alone.
- **Moods** (`setMusicMood`): `loading` (new: while the save loads; slow and gentle), `kitchen`, `cutting` (quieter, fewer notes, no chimes), `night` (Closing Time: slowest, sparse), `off` (intro / finale / THE LAST PAGE). One continuous loop through every mood change.
- **Loading music:** App asks for `loading` while the save is not there. Browsers keep a page silent until a tap, so: if the page already had one (`navigator.userActivation.hasBeenActive`: the portal's Play button or a tap while loading) it plays at once, otherwise at the first tap, and the loading screen says "🎵 tap for music" until then (no autoplay warning is ever logged: the context is not created before). The Music toggle in Settings still rules once the save is in.
- **Tests:** new `music-qa` (copyright, the piece, loading) and `tools/e2e/music.mjs` (a real browser: silent and no warning before a tap, the hint, the real output level 0.05-0.11 peak after the tap, still moving 40 s later, no console errors). `audio-qa` updated (fake context gained the convolver and compressor; M3 now checks the new score's seeding).
- **Not done:** a volume slider / separate music volume (the toggle only); I could not listen to it, so the levels (`MUSIC_LEVEL`) and the gains in `relaxComposer.ts` are set by measurement, not by ear: the developer should listen and say if it is too quiet / loud.

## NPC: nine new looks, guests walk around the tables, a little smaller (developer 2026-11-08, UNCOMMITTED)

- **Nine new customer sheets** (`NPC Assets/`: international-tourist-f/m, food-blogger-f/m, businessman, affluent-diner-f, family-mother/father/child) are cut by `scripts/make-npc-assets.py` (ids added; `family-mother` has its whole figure at alpha 230-249 and figures touching across rows, so `thresholds` cuts it by rectangles) into `public/people/<id>/a.webp` (+0.58 MB; the cast budget in `npc-sprites-qa` A4 is 3.6 MB now) and are guest looks in `peopleLooks.ts` (the dining room, the Second Floor and the walk-ins pick them). Their row 3 is not the old guest order (menu, hand, eat...): nothing reads it for guests (a guest sits from the back view, row 1), only the walk rows are used.
- **The first floor's 7 guests** are now a food blogger, a businessman and an affluent diner, and a family of four (father, mother, child, grandmother).
- **Walking** (`restaurant/guestWalk.ts`, `guestLife.ts`): the six artworks have their tables in different places and the old routes were measured on the first one, so guests crossed tables on the others. `STAGE_TABLES` has the table centres per artwork (front tables from `FLOOR_SEATS`, the rest measured on the art; the sofa-corner booths are obstacles only); a person never steps on a cloth, never through another table's chairs, and walks to their own chair through their own table's chairs. The route is found with A* on a half-percent grid and pulled straight. A chair that cannot be reached (the nine-table room is packed) is not used; when the room is packed to the counter (nine tables) nobody queues in the middle: they use the free lane at the right and the front lane. `RestaurantMap` takes `stage` (the router passes `kitchenMapArtStage`; without it the stage is read from the artwork file name).
- **Size:** a map person `clamp(29px, 9.6vw, 56px)` (was 10.5vw / 62 px), the dining-room guest 124% (was 135%).
- **Tests:** new `guest-walk-qa` (A the looks, B on all six artworks: no table top stepped on, only open floor, own chair, own counter spot, C size); `npc-sprites-qa` (budget, pool size), `bug-batch-qa` 8 (size) updated to the developer's new numbers. `oven-step-qa` E2 allows the new pure files (afterServe / celebrations / floorGuide); `npcpeople.mjs` P3 now asks for a 29 px box. Looked at in a real browser on the flourishing-cafe artwork (nobody on a table); the other five were checked on route overlays drawn over their art and by `guest-walk-qa`.

## Bugs and Issues doc, 11 items (developer 2026-11-08, committed ddbf78d)

Source: "Bugs and Issues KnifeCraft.docx". Tests: `bug-batch-qa` (rules), `tap-cut-qa` (cutting), browser `tools/e2e/bugbatch.mjs`.

1. **Restroom picture shakes when wiping starts:** the cleaning footer changed height when the "use the squeegee" hint appeared and when the status line wrapped, so the room (sized from the free height) rescaled. A fixed 32 px hint slot and a single-line status line (`AreaCleaningPrototype.tsx`); bugbatch B1 measures the stage box before / during / after.
2. **Paige is a popup:** the same card as the Hire popup (her round portrait, her words, the buttons) over a dimmed, blurred scrim (`AssistantHost.tsx`, `data-testid="assistant-scrim"`). What she points at is left bright by a spotlight cut-out with the ring on it; tapping the dark closes the tip (not the hire). No speech bubble any more.
3. **Congratulations:** `restaurant/celebrations.ts` (pure) + `common/CelebrationCard.tsx`: a ranking climb, a popularity rise of 3+ (checked after a level and after a night's close) or a spotless run of 2 gives ONE card with the facts and Paige's appreciation. Moves no money.
4. **First machine, step by step:** `restaurant/floorGuide.ts`, plan in docs/planning/SECOND-FLOOR-FIRST-MACHINE-PLAN-2026-11-08.md (a progress card then a Buy card with a hand; the first machine needs price + $300 everywhere it can be bought).
5. **Cutting speed:** a tap cut is 320 ms at 1.0 (was 455); every knife is at most 0.9 and the basic Chef's Knife has the top knife's 0.85 (272 ms); the tap buffer is 110 ms; the closest two cuts may be is 10% of the band (was 7%), so a slice and a chop are 8 cuts (were 12), a julienne 8 (was 10). Fever lights at x5 (was x10, now unreachable in one step).
6. **Tapping one spot, fast:** `CutGeometry.magnetToSlot`: a tap lands near the nearest free even slot and keeps 15% of its offset; the first cut goes exactly onto its slot; a tap on a cut slot goes exactly onto the next free one. Tapping the same spot over and over gives a perfect 100 evenness and consistency. Not applied to Chop (irregular on purpose) or Halve.
7. **2nd floor button:** `restaurantMap/UpstairsButton.tsx` + `upstairs.css`, a brass medallion with a stairway icon, "2nd Floor" and a note ("Money waiting", "Full · collect", "Buy a machine", "Save for a machine"), a breathing glow and a red chip (`$4.40`, "Full", "New!") when there is a reason to go up.
8. **NPC size:** a person on the map is 2/3 of the old size (`clamp(32px, 10.5vw, 62px)`, was `clamp(48px, 16vw, 92px)`), the dining-room guests 135% of the puppet box (was 190%); a little smaller again the same day: see the NPC section above.
9. **Seated guests face the table:** a guest sits in the front chair, so they are drawn from behind (the walk-up row, cut at the waist, chair hides the legs) in the dining room (`GuestPoses`) and on the map (`FloorLife` `back`). The sheets only draw seated poses facing the viewer.
10. **Names:** the 12 regulars are Emma, Lucas, Sofia, Oscar, Mia, Mateo, Yuki, Liam, Isabella, Felix, Chloé, Noah (display names only; the ids that name the portraits are unchanged).
11. **No "Next Customer" for the first 20 levels:** `restaurant/afterServe.ts`: the served card shows "Back to Kitchen" as the main button and up to three chips for what to do on the way (unpack the delivery, stock the shelves, the 2nd floor, clean, restock). The next customer waits in the Kitchen ("Open for service"; the level resumes with its paid orders counted). Level 21 and later are as before. `NEXT_CUSTOMER_HIDDEN_THROUGH_LEVEL` in `config/earlyLevels.ts`.

- **Re-baselined tests:** blacksmith (the cadence numbers), julienne-fix, phase3 (Fever x5), oven-step / next-kitchen-strip (the diff guards allow `campaignPlan.ts`), paige.mjs (the popup look), peelslow.mjs (the Level 2 seed needs a carrot), cashflow.mjs and every browser test that clicked "Next Customer" (they now accept "Back to Kitchen", the only way on in Levels 1-20).
- **QA suites failing before this commit (checked against a clean HEAD, 2026-11-08):** `journey-qa` J11 (`docs/planning/LEVEL-DISPOSITION-2026-10-31.md` needs `scripts/level-disposition.mts` re-run for the 91 levels), `phase11-qa` D1/D2/D5/D6 (cleaning written for Level 3; it now starts later), `service-flow-qa` S4. `assistant-qa` G6/H5 were updated to the popup rule (dimmed scrim, ivory card).
- **Browser tests still stale (not re-run or known to fail):** `day2.mjs` (Y1-Y5: written for cleaning at Level 3; it now starts with the night of Level 4), `dining.mjs` D5/D6, and the ones already listed as stale on 2026-11-07. A mid-level serve now lands in the Kitchen and needs "Open for service" again; tests that played several orders in a row on Levels 1-20 through "Next Customer" need that extra step.
